




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

abhidhammapiṭakai

paṭṭhānapāḷi

(dutiyau bhāgau)

dhammānulaumai

tikapaṭṭhānaṃ

6. vitak‍kattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (4)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (5)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| (6)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (7)

2. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau  ca kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (5)

3. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vicārau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca  savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca| (5)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca| (6)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (7)

4. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai  savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (7)

5. avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai  khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicārau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā; vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca| (5)

6. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ  khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca  vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (3)

7. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

8. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

9. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati  ārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

10. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vicārau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārau ca| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca| (5)

11. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| (3)

12. avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicārau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca| (4)

13. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ  khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ  khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

(dvai pac‍cayā sajjhāyamaggaina vibhattā, aivaṃ avasaisā vīsatipac‍cayā vibhajitabbā|)

vippayuttapac‍cayau

14. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau; vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ| vitak‍kau vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (6)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (7)

15. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, vicārau vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vitak‍kaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ vitak‍kaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| khandhā ca vicārau ca vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

16. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicāraṃ  paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicāraṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, vicāraṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vatthu khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vicārau, vicārau vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vatthu vicāraṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā  uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ vicāraṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ vicāraṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, vitak‍kau vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (5)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (6)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (7)

17. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau, savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, vitak‍kau vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca  paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (7)

18. avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai  khandhai ca…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| vitak‍kaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| vitak‍kaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicārau| vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ vitak‍kaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (5)

19. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau  khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

20. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

atthi-avigatapac‍cayā

21. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati atthipac‍cayā…pai॰… natthipac‍cayā, vigatapac‍cayā, avigatapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

22. haituyā  sattatiṃsa, ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā taivīsa, anantarai aikavīsa, samanantarai  aikavīsa, sahajātai sattatiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aṭṭhavīsa, nissayai sattatiṃsa, upanissayai aikavīsa, puraijātai aikādasa, āsaivanai aikādasa, kammai sattatiṃsa, vipākai sattatiṃsa, āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sattatiṃsa, sampayuttai aikavīsa, vippayuttai sattatiṃsa, atthiyā sattatiṃsa, natthiyā aikavīsa, vigatai aikavīsa, avigatai sattatiṃsa|

dukaṃ

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai sattatiṃsa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai gaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

23. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (6)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau  ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (7)

24. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā; vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau  khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai  vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (5)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca| (6)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (7)

25. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (7)

26. avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā; vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

27. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai  ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

28. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai savitak‍kasavicārai  khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca  mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

29. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā  – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārai  khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca vatthu; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

30. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca…pai॰… kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati  naārammaṇapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā  – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

31. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

32. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati  naadhipatipac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārā adhipati, vipākaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, vipākaṃ vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… satta|

33. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā, avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naanantarapac‍cayādi

34. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā (naārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

35. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā… satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati  napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau, avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā…pai॰… arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

36. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… avitak‍kaavicārai  khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā  – arūpai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ)| (7)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… satta|

37. avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ)| (7)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(napuraijātamūlakai yathā suddhikaṃ arūpaṃ, tathā arūpā kātabbā)|

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

38. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā…pai॰… naāsaivanapac‍cayā… satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… (saṃkhittaṃ)| (5)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(naāsaivanamūlakai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ vipākaina saha gacchantaina napuraijātasadisaṃ kātabbaṃ, avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattagacchantaina vipākau dassaitabbau|)

nakammapac‍cayau

39. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati  nakammapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

40. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā…pai॰… naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ…pai॰… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ…  utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… namaggapac‍cayā… nasampayuttapac‍cayā…|

navippayuttapac‍cayau

41. navippayuttapac‍cayā… arūpai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai paṭic‍ca vitak‍kau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vitak‍kau ca| (3)

42. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicārau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vicārau ca| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vicāraṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

naunatthi-nauvigatapac‍cayā

43. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

44. nahaituyā  taittiṃsa, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā sattatiṃsa, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai sattatiṃsa, napacchājātai sattatiṃsa, naāsaivanai sattatiṃsa, nakammai satta, navipākai taivīsa, naāhārai aikaṃ  naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai taittiṃsa, nasampayuttai satta, navippayuttai aikādasa, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

45. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai satta…pai॰… nauvigatai satta|

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

46. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cuddasa, anantarai samanantarai cuddasa, sahajātai taittiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai bāvīsa, nissayai taittiṃsa, upanissayai cuddasa, puraijātai cha, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, kammai taittiṃsa…pai॰… jhānai taittiṃsa, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai cuddasa, vippayuttai taittiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai taittiṃsa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā  kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

(sahajātavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisau kātabbau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

47. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā… satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… pañ‍ca (paṭic‍cavārasadisā)|

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā  cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā…pai॰… vicāraṃ pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicārau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… vicāraṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā  khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vitak‍kau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… vicāraṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vitak‍kau, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (6)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau  ca dhammā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (7)

48. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā …pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… (paṭhamaudāharaṇai pavattai paṭisandhikkhaṇai satta pañhā kātabbā)|

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau  …pai॰… vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vitak‍kañ‍ca  mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicārau (aivaṃ paṭisandhikkhaṇai cattārau)| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, vitak‍kañ‍ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (5)

(avasaisaisu dvīsu ghaṭanaisu pavatti paṭisandhi vitthāraitabbā|)

haitupac‍cayau|

(haitupac‍cayaṃ  anumaj‍jantaina pac‍cayavārau vitthāraitabbau| yathā paṭic‍cagaṇanā aivaṃ gaṇaitabbā| adhipatiyā sattatiṃsa, puraijātai ca āsaivanai ca aikavīsa, ayaṃ aittha visaisau|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

49. pac‍canīyai – nahaituyā taittiṃsa pañhā, sattasu ṭhānaisu satta  mauhā uddharitabbā mūlapadaisuyaiva| naārammaṇai satta cittasamuṭṭhānā uddharitabbā|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

50. savitak‍kasavicāramūlakā satta pañhā naadhipatiyā kātabbā|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā (yathā paṭic‍canayai tathā pañ‍ca pañhā kātabbā)|

51. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārā adhipati, vipākaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… vipākaṃ vicāraṃ pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārā adhipati, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā ca vicārau ca…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… vicāraṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, vipākaṃ vicāraṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā…pai॰… vipākaṃ vicāraṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vitak‍kau, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (6)

(paṭhamaghaṭanāyaṃ sampuṇṇā satta pañhā kātabbā|)

52. avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…|

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati, avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā  adhipati, vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā…pai॰… vipākaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…|

(paṭisandhikkhaṇai pañ‍ca pañhā kātabbā| yattha  avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ āgacchati tattha vipākaṃ kātabbaṃ| naadhipatimūlakai sattatiṃsa pañhā kātabbā|)

naanantarapac‍cayādi

53. naanantarampi naanantaraipi (ka॰) nasamanantarampi naañ‍ñamañ‍ñampi naupanissayampi satta pañhā rūpaṃyaiva| napuraijātai sattatiṃsa paṭic‍cavārapac‍canīyasadisaṃ| napacchājātai sattatiṃsa, naāsaivanaipi sadisaṃ| yattha avitak‍kavicāramattaupi āgacchati, tattha vipākā kātabbā|

nakammapac‍cayau

54. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… vitak‍kaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (2)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kaavicārā caitanā…pai॰… (paripuṇṇaṃ kātabbaṃ) savitak‍kasavicārau…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| avitak‍kavicāramattau…pai॰… vicāraṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| (3)

55. savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai  khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca  avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā| (1)

(navipākai sattatiṃsa pañhā kātabbā| naāhāra-naindriya-najhāna-namagga-nasampayuttanavippayutta-naunatthi-nauvigatapac‍cayā vitthāraitabbā|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

56. nahaituyā taittiṃsa, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā sattatiṃsa, naanantarai nasamanantarai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai sattatiṃsa, nakammai aikādasa, navipākai sattatiṃsa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai taittiṃsa, nasampayuttai satta, navippayuttai aikādasa, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

58. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai anantarai samanantarai cuddasa, sahajātai taittiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai bāvīsa, nissayai taittiṃsa, upanissayai puraijātai cuddasa, āsaivanai tairasa, kammai vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai taittiṃsa, maggai pañ‍ca, sampayuttai cuddasa, vippayuttai atthiyā taittiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai taittiṃsa|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

(nissayampi nin‍nānaṃ)

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

59. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vitak‍kau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

60. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau…pai॰… vitak‍kaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicārau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicārau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā  …pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā vicārau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

61. avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā  tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… vicāraṃ saṃsaṭṭhā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicāraṃ saṃsaṭṭhā…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vicārañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vitak‍kañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

(haitupac‍cayaṃ anumaj‍jantaina sabbai pac‍cayā vitthāraitabbā)|

suddhaṃ

62. haituyā aikādasa, ārammaṇai adhipatiyā anantarai samanantarai sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai upanissayai puraijātai āsaivanai kammai vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai vippayuttai atthiyā natthiyā vigatai avigatai sabbattha aikādasa|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

(pac‍canīyaṃ  kātabbaṃ asammauhantaina|)

63. nahaituyā cha, naadhipatiyā aikādasa, napuraijātai aikādasa, napacchājātai aikādasa, naāsaivanai aikādasa, nakammai satta, navipākai  aikādasa, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai cha, navippayuttai aikādasa|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

dukaṃ

64. haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā aikādasa…pai॰… navippayuttai aikādasa (saṃkhittaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

65. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… puraijātai cha, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, kammai cha…pai॰… jhānai cha, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai cha…pai॰… avigatai cha|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

6. sampayuttavārau

(sampayuttavāraupi vitthāraitabbau)|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

66. savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū vitak‍kassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā haitū kaṭattārūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū vitak‍kassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā haitū vitak‍kassa kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

67. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā haitū  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā haitū vicārassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā haitū vicārassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

68. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

69. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati; savitak‍kasavicārā  jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati; savitak‍kasavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau  anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina savitak‍kasavicāracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti; savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ārabbha avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati; savitak‍kasavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

70. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau  uppaj‍jati| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau  vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina avitak‍kavicāramattacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)
