avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| avitak‍kavicāramattai  khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

71. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa phalassa vicārassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina avitak‍kaavicāracittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā avitak‍kaavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| ariyā nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ  gautrabhussa vaudānassa savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa phalassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ … jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā avitak‍kaavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| ariyā nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa phalassa vitak‍kassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau…pai॰… vatthuṃ… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa phalassa vicārassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā avitak‍kaavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| ariyā nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa vitak‍kassa ca vaudānassa vitak‍kassa ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa vitak‍kassa ca āvaj‍janāya vitak‍kassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| sautaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbaṃ… vatthuṃ… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (5)

72. avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

73. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha avitak‍kaavicārā  khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

adhipatipac‍cayau

74. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| savitak‍kasavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| savitak‍kasavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati vitak‍kassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati  – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ  katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, savitak‍kasavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| sahajātādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

75. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā  vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati vicārassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (5)

76. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa phalassa vicārassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – avitak‍kaavicārā adhipati  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā avitak‍kaavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti, ariyā nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa vaudānassa savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā avitak‍kaavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati, ariyā nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa phalassa vitak‍kassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa phalassa vicārassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā avitak‍kaavicārā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti, ariyā nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa vitak‍kassa ca vaudānassa vitak‍kassa ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa  vitak‍kassa ca savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa vitak‍kassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… vatthuṃ… avitak‍kaavicārai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (5)

77. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattai  khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – avitak‍kavicāramattai khandhai ca vicārañ‍ca garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

78. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca garuṃ katvā vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – savitak‍kasavicārai khandhai ca vitak‍kañ‍ca garuṃ katvā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

79. savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa… vaudānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa… savitak‍kasavicārau maggau savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ phalaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa… anulaumaṃ savitak‍kasavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa vitak‍kassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa upapatticittassa…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā avitak‍kavicāramattassa vuṭṭhānassa vitak‍kassa ca…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa… gautrabhu avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa… vaudānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa… anulaumaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattāya phalasamāpattiyā vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ avitak‍kaavicārassa upapatticittassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā pañ‍can‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā avitak‍kaavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa vicārassa ca…pai॰… dutiyassa jhānassa parikammaṃ dutiyai jhānai vicārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tatiyassa jhānassa parikammaṃ  …pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā parikammaṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… gautrabhu avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca… vaudānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa  vicārassa ca… anulaumaṃ avitak‍kaavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa upapatticittassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā avitak‍kavicāramattassa vuṭṭhānassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu avitak‍kavicāramattassa  maggassa vicārassa ca… vaudānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa vicārassa ca… anulaumaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattāya phalasamāpattiyā vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa vitak‍kassa ca… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa vitak‍kassa ca… gautrabhu savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca… vaudānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca… savitak‍kasavicārau maggau savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa vitak‍kassa ca… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ phalaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa vitak‍kassa ca… anulaumaṃ savitak‍kasavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

80. avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vitak‍kau pacchimassa pacchimassa vitak‍kassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau maggau avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ phalaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vitak‍kau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ cuticittaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā savitak‍kasavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa vicārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ cuticittaṃ vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa upapatticittassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau maggau avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa vicārassa ca… avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ phalaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vitak‍kau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ cuticittaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa upapatticittassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā savitak‍kasavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

81. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicārau pacchimassa pacchimassa vicārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ  avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārau maggau avitak‍kaavicārassa phalassa… avitak‍kaavicāraṃ phalaṃ avitak‍kaavicārassa phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ avitak‍kaavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa  upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ vicārau ca āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ savitak‍kasavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicārau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa upapatticittassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa vuṭṭhānassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niraudhā  vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattāya phalasamāpattiyā vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicārau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa upapatticittassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā avitak‍kavicāramattassa vuṭṭhānassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattāya phalasamāpattiyā vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ cuticittaṃ vicārau  ca savitak‍kasavicārassa upapatticittassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ bhavaṅgañ‍ca vicārau ca āvaj‍janāya vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ savitak‍kasavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

82. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ cuticittañ‍ca vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ bhavaṅgañ‍ca vicārau ca āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā  avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau  maggau ca vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ phalañ‍ca vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca pacchimassa pacchimassa vicārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ cuticittañ‍ca vicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau maggau ca vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa vicārassa  ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ phalañ‍ca vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa phalassa ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ cuticittañ‍ca vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa upapatticittassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ bhavaṅgañ‍ca vicārau ca āvaj‍janāya vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

83. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca  pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca gautrabhussa… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca vaudānassa… gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa… savitak‍kasavicārau maggau ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ phalañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā  savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca pacchimassa pacchimassa vitak‍kassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ cuticittañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa … anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ cuticittañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa upapatticittassa vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āvaj‍janā ca vitak‍kau ca pañ‍can‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa vuṭṭhānassa ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dutiyassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca dutiyai jhānai vicārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tatiyassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa  parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca  …pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ cuticittañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa upapatticittassa ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa vuṭṭhānassa ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa  maggassa ca vicārassa  ca… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya phalasamāpattiyā ca vicārassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca gautrabhussa ca vitak‍kassa ca… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca vaudānassa ca vitak‍kassa ca… gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa ca maggassa ca vitak‍kassa ca… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa ca vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| savitak‍kasavicārau maggau ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa ca vitak‍kassa ca … savitak‍kasavicāraṃ phalañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa phalassa ca vitak‍kassa ca… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya phalasamāpattiyā ca vitak‍kassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

samanantarapac‍cayau

84. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarapac‍cayaupi samanantarapac‍cayaupi sadisau)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

85. savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vitak‍kassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā  khandhā vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (7)

86. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vitak‍kau savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vitak‍kau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vitak‍kau savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

87. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārau  aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… vicārau kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… vicārau vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu vicārassa…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtā kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu vitak‍kassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ  kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

88. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vatthu ca vitak‍kassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

89. avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vatthu ca vicārassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā ca vicārau ca aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai  khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca  sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

90. savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

91. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

92. savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vitak‍kassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau  aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vitak‍kassa ca vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca  vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

93. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vitak‍kau savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārassa ca vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ  vicārassa ca vatthussa  ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

94. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa  ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… vicārau vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu vicārassa…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  vatthu vitak‍kassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa  añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

95. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vatthu ca vitak‍kassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

96. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vatthu ca vicārassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

97. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

98. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ) satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ) pañ‍ca|

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca  rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā  ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau  avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau…pai॰…| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu…pai॰…| (5)

99. savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (pavattipi, paṭisandhipi dīpaitabbā)| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vatthu ca vitak‍kassa…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau  ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

100. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau  khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vatthu ca vicārassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭisandhikāni cattāri| saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca  dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

101. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa  …pai॰… avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa…pai॰… tīṇi|

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa…pai॰… avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (dvai vārā vitthāraitabbā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

102. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, savitak‍kasavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, savitak‍kasavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ…pai॰… pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  sīlaṃ …pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ…pai॰… savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicāraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kaavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kaavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā avitak‍kaavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca… kāyikassa sukhassa kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

103. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… vitak‍kaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya  saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, savitak‍kasavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… vitak‍kaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, savitak‍kasavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kaavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… vitak‍kaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kaavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca… kāyikassa sukhassa kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa  ca dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

104. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kaavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… vicāraṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kaavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kaavicārā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… vicārau… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ avitak‍kaavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya… vicārassa… kāyikassa sukhassa kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, savitak‍kasavicāraṃ jhānaṃ  uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… vicāraṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, savitak‍kasavicāraṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| avitak‍kaavicārā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau  …pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kaavicāraṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kaavicāraṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| avitak‍kaavicārā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kaavicārā  saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kaavicārā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa…pai॰… patthanāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

105. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau  …pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca… kāyikassa sukhassa kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā vicārau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

106. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā  saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya sīlassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā  saddhā sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanā vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā…pai॰… patthanā vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca… kāyikassa sukhassa kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā…pai॰… patthanā  vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya vicārassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – savitak‍kasavicārā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya vitak‍kassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

puraijātapac‍cayau

107. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…  vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ…  jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti  abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

108. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā ca vicārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa  dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

109. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa… vaudānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa vitak‍kassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vaudānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dutiyassa jhānassa parikammaṃ dutiyai jhānai vicārassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| tatiyassa jhānassa parikammaṃ tatiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… catutthassa jhānassa parikammaṃ catutthassa jhānassa…pai॰…  ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ dibbassa cakkhussa…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā parikammaṃ dibbāya sautadhātuyā…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… gautrabhu avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca… vaudānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu avitak‍kavicāramattassa  maggassa vicārassa ca… vaudānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ  vitak‍kassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa vitak‍kassa ca… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa vitak‍kassa ca… gautrabhu savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca… vaudānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

110. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vitak‍kau pacchimassa pacchimassa vitak‍kassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vitak‍kau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa vicārassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa  ca dhammassa  āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vitak‍kau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

111. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicārau pacchimassa pacchimassa vicārassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicārau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicārau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

112. avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa  dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca pacchimassa pacchimassa vicārassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)
