113. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca gautrabhussa… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca vaudānassa… gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca pacchimassa pacchimassa vitak‍kassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍ka vicāramattassa maggassa… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dutiyassa jhānassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau  ca dutiyai jhānai vicārassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca anāgataṃsañāṇassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kaavicārassa maggassa vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa  parikammañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa jhānassa vicārassa ca… gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa vicārassa ca… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa maggassa vicārassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – purimā purimā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca gautrabhussa vitak‍kassa ca… anulaumañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca vaudānassa vitak‍kassa ca… gautrabhu ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca… vaudānañ‍ca vitak‍kau ca savitak‍kasavicārassa maggassa vitak‍kassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

kammapac‍cayau

114. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vipākānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vitak‍kassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vitak‍kassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vipākassa vitak‍kassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā kaṭattā rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vipākānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vipākānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vitak‍kassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vipākassa  vitak‍kassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vicākānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca  rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – savitak‍kasavicārā caitanā vipākānaṃ savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

115. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā  caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā vipākānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā vipākassa vicārassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā vipākassa vicārassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā  – avitak‍kavicāramattā caitanā vipākānaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – avitak‍kaavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca  rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – avitak‍kaavicārā caitanā vipākānaṃ avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

116. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vitak‍kassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…|

(savitak‍kasavicāramūlakā sattapi pañhā paripuṇṇā|)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau…pai॰…|

(avitak‍kavicāramattamūlakā  pañ‍ca pañhā kātabbā, vipākanti niyāmaitabbā|)

117. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā …pai॰… vipākau vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

118. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau savitak‍kasavicārau aikau  khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

āhārapac‍cayau

119. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

(savitak‍kasavicāramūlakā iminā kāraṇaina satta pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā āhārā vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā āhārā  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

indriyapac‍cayau

120. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā indriyā vitak‍kassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (7)

(savitak‍kasavicāramūlakā satta pañhā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā|)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā indriyā vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

jhānapac‍cayau

121. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (7)

(savitak‍kasavicāramūlakā satta pañhā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā|)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (5)

(avitak‍kavicāramattamūlakā  pañ‍ca pañhā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā|)

avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau kaṭattārūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau vatthussa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

122. avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattāni jhānaṅgāni vicārau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattāni jhānaṅgāni vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattāni jhānaṅgāni vicārau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattāni jhānaṅgāni vicārau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni jhānaṅgāni vitak‍kau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni jhānaṅgāni vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni  jhānaṅgāni vitak‍kau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

maggapac‍cayau

123. savitak‍kasavicārau  dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni maggaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…|

(savitak‍kasavicāramūlakā satta pañhā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā|)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattāni  maggaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…|

(avitak‍kavicāramattamūlakā pañ‍ca pañhā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā|)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārāni maggaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni maggaṅgāni vitak‍kau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārāni maggaṅgāni vitak‍kau ca sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

sampayuttapac‍cayau

124. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ  sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vitak‍kassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca  sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

125. avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vitak‍kau savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

126. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa  dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

vippayuttapac‍cayau

127. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vitak‍kau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vitak‍kau kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

128. avitak‍kaavicārau  dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā  – avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicārau kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; vicārau vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; vatthu vicārassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā ca vicārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ  khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

129. avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau  ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayau

130. savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā vitak‍kassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

(savitak‍kasavicāramūlakai avasaisā  pañhā sahajātapac‍cayasadisā|)

131. avitak‍kavicāramattau  dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vitak‍kau savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vitak‍kau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā vicārassa kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; vitak‍kau kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

(avitak‍kavicāramattamūlakā pañ‍ca pañhā| avasaisā sahajātapac‍cayasadisā|)

132. avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā  dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… vicārau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  avitak‍kaavicārau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; vicārau vatthussa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu vicārassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtā kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā  saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu avitak‍kaavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kaavicārā khandhā ca vicārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu  savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha  vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha vitak‍kau uppaj‍jati| vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca  rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vicārau avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ  kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu avitak‍kavicāramattānaṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca uppaj‍janti| vatthu savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

133. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārau  aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vatthu ca vitak‍kassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vatthu ca vitak‍kassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā  – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa ca avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau  ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vitak‍kassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

134. avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca savitak‍kasavicārānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ… avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātā  – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātau – vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vatthu ca vicārassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vitak‍kau ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vatthu ca vicārassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vicārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – avitak‍kavicāramattā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

avitak‍kavicāramattau  ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā avitak‍kavicāramattassa ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vicārau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vicārau ca…pai॰… avitak‍kavicāramattau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vicārassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

135. savitak‍kasavicārau  ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā avitak‍kaavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau  ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – savitak‍kasavicārā khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa  ca avitak‍kaavicārassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vitak‍kau ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

136. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca avitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – savitak‍kasavicārau aikau khandhau ca vitak‍kau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

138. haituyā  aikādasa, ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā taivīsa, anantarai pañ‍cavīsa, samanantarai pañ‍cavīsa, sahajātai tiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aṭṭhavīsa, nissayai tiṃsa, upanissayai pañ‍cavīsa, puraijātai pañ‍ca, pacchājātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai aikavīsa, kammai aikādasa, vipākai aikavīsa, āhārai aikādasa, indriyai aikādasa, jhānai aikavīsa, maggai sauḷasa, sampayuttai aikādasa, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā tiṃsa, natthiyā pañ‍cavīsa, vigatai pañ‍cavīsa, avigatai tiṃsa|

(ghaṭanā kusalattikasadisāyaiva| pañhāvāragaṇanaṃ aivaṃ asammauhantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

144. savitak‍kasavicārau ca avitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā savitak‍kasavicārassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍canīyuddhārau|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

suddhaṃ

145. nahaituyā pañ‍catiṃsa, naārammaṇai pañ‍catiṃsa, naadhipatiyā pañ‍catiṃsa, naanantarai pañ‍catiṃsa, nasamanantarai pañ‍catiṃsa, nasahajātai aikūnatiṃsa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikūnatiṃsa, nanissayai aikūnatiṃsa, naupanissayai catuttiṃsa, napuraijātai pañ‍catiṃsa, napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai pañ‍catiṃsa, nasampayuttai aikūnatiṃsa, navippayuttai sattavīsa, nauatthiyā sattavīsa, naunatthiyā pañ‍catiṃsa, nauvigatai pañ‍catiṃsa, nauavigatai sattavīsa|

(pac‍canīyaṃ gaṇaintaina imāni padāni anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbāni|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

146. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai aikādasa, naadhipatiyā aikādasa, naanantarai nasamanantarai aikādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai aikādasa, napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai  naindriyai najhānai namaggai sabbai aikādasa, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai satta, naunatthiyā aikādasa, nauvigatai aikādasa|

(anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā iminā kāraṇaina gaṇaitabbā|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

147. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā taivīsa, anantarai pañ‍cavīsa, samanantarai pañ‍cavīsa, sahajātai tiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aṭṭhavīsa, nissayai tiṃsa, upanissayai pañ‍cavīsa, puraijātai pañ‍ca, pacchājātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai aikavīsa, kammai aikādasa, vipākai aikavīsa, āhārai aikādasa, indriyai aikādasa, jhānai aikavīsa, maggai sauḷasa, sampayuttai aikādasa, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā tiṃsa, natthiyā pañ‍cavīsa, vigatai pañ‍cavīsa, avigatai tiṃsa|

(pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

vitak‍kattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. pītittikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. pītisahagataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (2)

pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca tayau khandhā  …pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (3)

2. sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai  paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (1)

sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (2)

sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhasahagataṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (3)

3. upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upaikkhāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai  paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (2)

pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

4. pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā… (paṭisandhikkhaṇai natthi) anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… (puraijātai paṭisandhikkhaṇai natthi) āsaivanapac‍cayā… (āsaivanai vipākaṃ natthi) kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhāra…pai॰… indriya… jhāna… magga… sampayutta… vippayutta… atthi… natthi… vigata… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

5. haituyā  dasa, ārammaṇai dasa, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai samanantarai sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai upanissayai puraijātai āsaivanai kammai vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai vippayuttai atthiyā natthiyā vigatai avigatai sabbattha dasa|

(aivaṃ anulaumagaṇanā gaṇaitabbā|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

6. pītisahagataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (2)

pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pītisahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (3)

7. sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (1)

sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai  khandhā| (2)

sukhasahagataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ sukhasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (3)

8. upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ  upaikkhāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai upaikkhāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

9. pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (2)

pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca  aikau khandhau| (3)

naadhipati-naāsaivanapac‍cayā

10. pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (naadhipatipaṭisandhikkhaṇai paripuṇṇaṃ)… napuraijātapac‍cayā… (‘‘arūpai’’ti niyāmaitabbaṃ ‘‘paṭisandhikkhaṇai’’ti ca) napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā|

nakammapac‍cayau

11. pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – pītisahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca pītisahagatā caitanā|

pītisahagataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – pītisahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatā caitanā|

(iminā kāraṇaina dasa pañhā vitthāraitabbā|)

navipākapac‍cayau

12. pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā…pai॰… (paripuṇṇaṃ, paṭisandhi natthi)|

najhānapac‍cayādi

13. sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (1)

upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – catuviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| (1)

(namaggapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayasadisaṃ| mauhau natthi| navippayuttapac‍cayā paripuṇṇaṃ arūpapañhamaiva|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

dukaṃ

(anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ vitthāraina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|
