dukaṃ

16. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dasa, anantarai dasa, samanantarai dasa, sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai  upanissayai puraijātai āsaivanai kammai vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai sabbai dasa, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai dasa, vippayuttai atthiyā natthiyā vigatai avigatai sabbai dasa|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

17. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – pītisahagatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

18. sukhasahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa…pai॰… (sukhamūlai tīṇi)|

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – upaikkhāsahagatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca haitū sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

19. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pītisahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati, pītisahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā taṃ pītisahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā pītisahagataina cittaina pītisahagatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| pītisahagatai khandhai pītisahagataina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha pītisahagatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| pītisahagatai khandhai ārabbha pītisahagatā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

pītisahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ sukhasahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati, pītisahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā taṃ sukhasahagataina  cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā sukhasahagataina cittaina pītisahagatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| pītisahagatai khandhai sukhasahagataina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha sukhasahagatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| pītisahagatai khandhai ārabbha sukhasahagatā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

pītisahagatau  dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati, pītisahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā taṃ upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina pītisahagatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| pītisahagatai khandhai upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha upaikkhāsahagatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina pītisahagatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti, pītisahagatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatai khandhai ārabbha upaikkhāsahagatā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina  pac‍cavaikkhati, pītisahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā taṃ pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina pītisahagatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| pītisahagatai khandhai pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| pītisahagatai khandhai ārabbha pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| (4)

20. sukhasahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sukhasahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhasahagatai khandhai ārabbha pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| (4)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati, upaikkhāsahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā taṃ upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina upaikkhāsahagatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| upaikkhāsahagatai khandhai upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau  vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha upaikkhāsahagatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina upaikkhāsahagatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagatai khandhai ārabbha upaikkhāsahagatā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ  samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati, upaikkhāsahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā phalā vuṭṭhahitvā, taṃ pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina upaikkhāsahagatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| upaikkhāsahagatai khandhai pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca  cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti assādainti  abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| upaikkhāsahagatai khandhai ārabbha pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

21. pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā…pai॰… pītisahagatai ca sukhasahagatai ca khandhai upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha upaikkhāsahagatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina pītisahagatasukhasahagatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatai ca sukhasahagatai ca khandhai ārabbha upaikkhāsahagatā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (4)

adhipatipac‍cayau

22. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – pītisahagataina  cittaina dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā  pītisahagataina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pītisahagatā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā pītisahagataina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| pītisahagatai khandhai pītisahagataina cittaina garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā pītisahagatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – pītisahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – pītisahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – pītisahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

23. sukhasahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ datvā…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – sukhasahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhasahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – sukhasahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhasahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

sukhasahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – sukhasahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

24. upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – upaikkhāsahagatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati (saṃkhittaṃ)| (2)

upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati (saṃkhittaṃ)| (4)

25. pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā sukhasahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati (saṃkhittaṃ)| (3)

pītisahagatau  ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati  …pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

anantarapac‍cayau

26. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (iminā kāraṇaina sabbaisaṃ padānaṃ pac‍cayauti dīpaitabbau)| anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ pītisahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatassa gautrabhussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatassa vaudānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagataṃ cuticittaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagatā vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu kiriyamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ bhavaṅgaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa bhavaṅgassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ kusalākusalaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa vuṭṭhānassa… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca gautrabhussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

27. sukhasahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā sukhasahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatassa gautrabhussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhasahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhasahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā sukhasahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhasahagataṃ anulaumaṃ pītisahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhasahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sukhasahagataṃ cuticittaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagataṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipākamanaudhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagatā vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu kiriyamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau sukhasahagataṃ bhavaṅgaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa bhavaṅgassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagataṃ kusalākusalaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa vuṭṭhānassa… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sukhasahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā sukhasahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhasahagataṃ anulaumaṃ pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

28. upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā upaikkhāsahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ upaikkhāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… āvaj‍janā pañ‍can‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upaikkhāsahagataṃ anulaumaṃ upaikkhāsahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upaikkhāsahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – upaikkhāsahagataṃ cuticittaṃ pītisahagatassa upapatticittassa…pai॰… āvaj‍janā pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… vipākamanaudhātu pītisahagatāya vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ bhavaṅgaṃ pītisahagatassa bhavaṅgassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ kusalākusalaṃ pītisahagatassa vuṭṭhānassa… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pītisahagatāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

(tāniyaiva ca gamanāni niyāmaitabbāni|)

29. pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca cuticittaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa upapatticittassa…pai॰… pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya…pai॰… pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu kiriyamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca bhavaṅgaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa bhavaṅgassa…pai॰… pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca kusalākusalaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa vuṭṭhānassa… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau  ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā  ca sukhasahagatā ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ…pai॰… pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca anulaumaṃ pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

samanantarapac‍cayau

30. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

31. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (paṭic‍casadisaṃ sahajātai dasa pañhā)|

añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissayapac‍cayā

32. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (dasa pañhā kātabbā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

33. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, pītisahagataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagataṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ  upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati…pai॰… mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagataṃ rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, pītisahagataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| pītisahagataina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati, sandhiṃ chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā pītisahagatāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagataṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sukhasahagataina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau  dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā upaikkhāsahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā  saddhā…pai॰… patthanā pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

34. sukhasahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – sukhasahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sukhasahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ sukhasahagataṃ  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| sukhasahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sukhasahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhasahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – sukhasahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sukhasahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| sukhasahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pītisahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhasahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – sukhasahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sukhasahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| sukhasahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upaikkhāsahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sukhasahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau  …pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – sukhasahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sukhasahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| sukhasahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

35. upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| upaikkhāsahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| upaikkhāsahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā upaikkhāsahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina  cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| upaikkhāsahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| upaikkhāsahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā pītisahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| upaikkhāsahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| upaikkhāsahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| upaikkhāsahagataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| upaikkhāsahagatā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

36. pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca saddhā…pai॰… patthanā pītisahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā sukhasahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca saddhaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya sukhasahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā  ca saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhasahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca saddhaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti  …pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca sīlaṃ…pai॰…  patthanaṃ upanissāya upaikkhāsahagataina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca saddhā…pai॰… patthanā upaikkhāsahagatāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca saddhaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca jhānaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| pītisahagataina ca sukhasahagataina ca cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati, sandhiṃ chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti| pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca saddhā…pai॰… patthanā pītisahagatāya ca sukhasahagatāya ca saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

37. pītisahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ pītisahagatassa gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ  khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatassa gautrabhussa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ anulaumaṃ sukhasahagatassa vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pītisahagataṃ gautrabhu sukhasahagatassa maggassa… pītisahagataṃ vaudānaṃ sukhasahagatassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… pītisahagataṃ vaudānaṃ pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

38. sukhasahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ| pītinayaṃ passitvā kātabbaṃ)|

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – purimā purimā upaikkhāsahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ upaikkhāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ vaudānaṃ upaikkhāsahagatassa maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

39. pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa…pai॰… sukhasahagatassa  dhammassa…pai॰… pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pītisahagatā ca sukhasahagatā ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca vaudānaṃ pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kammapac‍cayau

40. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā nānākhaṇikā (syā॰ ka॰)| sahajātā – pītisahagatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatā  caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – pītisahagatā caitanā vipākānaṃ pītisahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – pītisahagatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – pītisahagatā caitanā vipākānaṃ sukhasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – pītisahagatā caitanā vipākānaṃ upaikkhāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – pītisahagatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  …pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – pītisahagatā caitanā vipākānaṃ pītisahagatānañ‍ca sukhasahagatānañ‍ca khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

41. sukhasahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa (cattāripi gaṇanāni passitvā kātabbāni)|

42. upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā…pai॰…|

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – upaikkhāsahagatā caitanā…pai॰…|

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – upaikkhāsahagatā caitanā…pai॰…|

upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – upaikkhāsahagatā caitanā…pai॰…| (4)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa (cattāri  kātabbāni, pītisahagataṃ anumaj‍jantaina vibhajitabbaṃ)| (4)

vipākapac‍cayau

43. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pītisahagatau vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai pītisahagatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…|

(yathā  paṭic‍cavārai haitupac‍cayai aivaṃ vitthāraitabbā dasa pañhā|)

āhārapac‍cayādi

44. pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (dasa pañhā vitthāraitabbā) natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (natthipi vigatampi anantarasadisaṃ) avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

45. haituyā dasa, ārammaṇai sauḷasa, adhipatiyā sauḷasa, anantarai sauḷasa, samanantarai sauḷasa, sahajātai dasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nissayai dasa, upanissayai sauḷasa, āsaivanai dasa, kammai sauḷasa, vipākai dasa, āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai atthiyā dasa, natthiyā sauḷasa, vigatai sauḷasa, avigatai dasa|

(kusalattikaṃ anulaumaṃ anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

46. pītisahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

47. sukhasahagatau dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhasahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhasahagatau dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sukhasahagatau  dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

48. upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau sukhasahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

49. pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā sukhasahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā upaikkhāsahagatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pītisahagatau ca sukhasahagatau ca dhammā pītisahagatassa ca sukhasahagatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

50. nahaituyā  sauḷasa, naārammaṇai naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai nasahajātai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nanissayai naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai nasampayuttai navippayuttai nauatthiyā naunatthiyā nauvigatai nauavigatai sabbattha sauḷasa|

(pac‍canīyaṃ anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

dukaṃ

51. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai dasa, naadhipatiyā dasa, naanantarai nasamanantarai naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai navippayuttai naunatthiyā nauvigatai sabbattha dasa|

(anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

dukaṃ

52. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai sauḷasa, adhipatiyā anantarai samanantarai sauḷasa, sahajātai dasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nissayai dasa, upanissayai sauḷasa, āsaivanai dasa, kammai sauḷasa, vipākai dasa, āhārai dasa, indriyai dasa, jhānai dasa, maggai dasa, sampayuttai dasa, atthiyā dasa, natthiyā sauḷasa, vigatai sauḷasa, avigatai dasa|

(pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pītittikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. dassanainapahātabbattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

2. bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

3. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai  khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

4. dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

5. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

6. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

7. dassanaina  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantara-samanantarapac‍cayā

8. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā (ārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

9. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

10. dassanaina  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

bhāvanāya  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

11. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vatthu ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vatthu ca, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā|

nissayapac‍cayādi

12. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nissayapac‍cayā… (haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ) upanissayapac‍cayā… tīṇi… puraijātapac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭisandhi natthi)… āsaivanapac‍cayā… (vipākapaṭisandhi natthi) kammapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ| ajjhattikā ca asañ‍ñasattānañ‍ca mahābhūtā)|

vipākapac‍cayau

13. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca  tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

āhārapac‍cayādi

14. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ, ajjhattikā mahābhūtā ca āhārasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca)… indriyapac‍cayā (kammapac‍cayasadisaṃ)… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā (haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ)… sampayuttapac‍cayā (ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ)… vippayuttapac‍cayā (kusalattikai vippayuttapac‍cayasadisaṃ)… atthipac‍cayā (sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ)… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

15. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava (imāni padāni anumaj‍jantaina anulaumaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

16. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

17. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

18. dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā  – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

19. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… (paripuṇṇaṃ haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ) naanantarapac‍cayā…  nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

20. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau…pai॰… arūpai bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

21. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ  khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

22. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā|

nakammapac‍cayau

23. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

24. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (naadhipatipac‍cayasadisaṃ, paṭisandhi natthi)|

naāhārapac‍cayau

25. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

naindriyapac‍cayau

26. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhāna… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ|

najhānapac‍cayau

27. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| utusamuṭṭhānaṃ, asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

namaggapac‍cayau

28. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

nasampayuttapac‍cayau

29. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nasampayuttapac‍cayā (naārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

navippayuttapac‍cayau

30. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… navippayuttapac‍cayā  – arūpai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naunatthi-nauvigatapac‍cayā

31. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya…pai॰… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā (naārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

32. nahaituyā tīṇi, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

(ñatvā gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

33. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

(aivaṃ anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

34. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi|

(aivaṃ anumaj‍jantaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

1-4. pac‍cayānulaumādi

35. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai sahajātā…pai॰…|

(sahajātavārau paṭic‍cavārasadisau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

36. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya… tīṇi|

37. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

38. dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

39. bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

40. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā  – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā| (3)

41. dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayādi

42. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… (paripuṇṇaṃ, paṭisandhi natthi) anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā (ārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

43. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ  aikaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā|

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā (avasaisā haitupac‍cayasadisā)|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayādi

44. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi)… āsaivanapac‍cayā… (paṭisandhi natthi, vipākañ‍ca) kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

45. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai  satta, kammai sattarasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

46. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (3)

47. dassanaina  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

48. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

49. dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

50. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā  (sahajātasadisaṃ)… naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

51. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

52. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ, asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

53. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā|

nakammapac‍cayau

54. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā| (3)

55. dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya  pahātabbai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

56. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ, paṭisandhi natthi), naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā rūpajīvitindriyaṃ… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… nasampayuttapac‍cayā, navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… arūpai bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina…pai॰… arūpai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

57. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

58. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai  satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

59. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai satta, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai satta, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai satta, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai cha, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai satta, atthiyā satta, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai satta (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pac‍cayavārau|

4. nissayavārau

(nissayavārau  pac‍cayasadisau kātabbau|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

60. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

61. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā (sabbāni padāni vitthāraitabbāni tīṇi, tīṇi)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

62. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

63. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

64. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā… nakammapac‍cayā… navipākapac‍cayā… – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya…pai॰… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau…pai॰… navippayuttapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

65. nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

66. haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

67. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai  tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

saṃsaṭṭhavārau|

6. sampayuttavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumādi

68. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ sampayuttau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (sampayuttavārau saṃsaṭṭhavārasadisau)|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

69. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

70. bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau…pai॰… dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbā haitū  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

71. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau  rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, vicikicchaṃ ārabbha vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| (1)
