dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā dassanaina pahātabbai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| dassanaina pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti| caitaupariyañāṇaina dassanaina pahātabbacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

72. bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā bhāvanāya pahātabbai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| bhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina bhāvanāya pahātabbacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

73. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā  maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha  dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… jhānai parihīnai vippaṭisārissa dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha  dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai assādaiti…pai॰… taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

74. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati  – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati  – dassanaina pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – dassanaina pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

75. bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa  dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – bhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

76. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti  abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| pubbai…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

77. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ  pacchimānaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa…pai॰… āvaj‍janā dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayau

78. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa  samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

79. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

80. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa…pai॰… bhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa…pai॰… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

81. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…  vatthu…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

82. dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

upanissayapac‍cayau

83. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati, adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… diṭṭhi… patthanā dassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa.. dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… diṭṭhi… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

84. bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya  pahātabbau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… patthanā bhāvanāya pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… patthanā dassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sakabhaṇḍai chandarāgau parabhaṇḍai chandarāgassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sakapariggahai chandarāgau parapariggahai chandarāgassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau…  patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

85. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ… sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ… upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau … pañ‍ñā… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ… saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ dassanaina pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya  mānaṃ jappaiti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ… upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti| saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayau

86. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā  uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati…pai॰…  vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati… uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati… bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, sautaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

87. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

88. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ…pai॰… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā bhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ…pai॰… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ…pai॰… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

89. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

90. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

āhārapac‍cayādi

91. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ) kabaḷīkārau… satta pañhā… indriyapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… cakkhundriyañ‍ca…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca  …pai॰… satta pañhā jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

92. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (idampi dassanaina sadisaṃ)|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ vatthu dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa  dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

atthipac‍cayau

93. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa… tīṇi (dassanaina sadisaṃ kātabbaṃ)|

94. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā  vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, sautaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbā  khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti…pai॰… vatthu dassanaina pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

95. dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – dassanaina pahātabbau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbassa…pai॰… (dvai pañhā kātabbā)|

natthivigatāvigatapac‍cayā

96. dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa  natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

97. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai aṭṭha, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

98. dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

99. bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

100. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

101. dassanaina  pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

102. nahaituyā  cuddasa, naārammaṇai cuddasa, naadhipatiyā cuddasa, naanantarai cuddasa, nasamanantarai cuddasa, nasahajātai dasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nanissayai dasa, naupanissayai cuddasa, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai cuddasa, naāsaivanai cuddasa, nakammai cuddasa, navipākai cuddasa, naāhārai cuddasa, naindriyai cuddasa, najhānai cuddasa, namaggai cuddasa, nasampayuttai dasa, navippayuttai  aṭṭha, nauatthiyā aṭṭha, naunatthiyā cuddasa, nauvigatai cuddasa, nauavigatai aṭṭha (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

103. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai  satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai satta, navipākai satta, naāhārai satta, naindriyai satta, najhānai satta, namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

104. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aṭṭha, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ )|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

dassanainapahātabbattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. dassanainapahātabbahaitukattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

2. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā  uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

3. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā  cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (5)

4. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

5. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

6. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā ca mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā ca mauhau ca| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi| (dassanaina sadisaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ|)

7. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

8. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

9. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi (haitusadisā)|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… tīṇi (haitusadisā, adhipatiyā mauhau natthi)|

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

10. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantara-samanantarapac‍cayā

11. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā| samanantarapac‍cayā (ārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

12. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca mauhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā  – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

13. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati  sahajātapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā  – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

14. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ| haitusadisaṃ kātabbaṃ)| (3)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayādi

15. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā, nissayapac‍cayā, upanissayapac‍cayā, puraijātapac‍cayā, āsaivanapac‍cayā, kammapac‍cayā, vipākapac‍cayā, āhārapac‍cayā, indriyapac‍cayā, jhānapac‍cayā, maggapac‍cayā, sampayuttapac‍cayā, vippayuttapac‍cayā, atthipac‍cayā, natthipac‍cayā, vigatapac‍cayā, avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

16. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai aikādasa, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai aikādasa, samanantarai aikādasa, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai aikādasa, puraijātai aikādasa, āsaivanai aikādasa, kammai sattarasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai aikādasa, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā aikādasa, vigatai aikādasa, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

17. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

18. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

19. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau  dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

20. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… (sahajātasadisaṃ) naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

21. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau; dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā  uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ… tīṇi (dassanaina sadisaṃ)|

22. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā, naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ  mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

23. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā (imaipi dvai kātabbā)|

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

24. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā|

nakammapac‍cayau

25. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā| (3)

26. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

27. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi)|

naāhārapac‍cayādi

28. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ…  utusamuṭṭhānaṃ, asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… (mahābhūtā kātabbā) namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… nasampayuttapac‍cayā|

navippayuttapac‍cayādi

29. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai bhāvanāya… tīṇi|

30. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

31. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

32. nahaituyā  tīṇi, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai tairasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai aikādasa, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

33. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai tairasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai aikādasa, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

34. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai dvai, sampayuttai  tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

(sahajātavārau paṭic‍cavārasadisau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

35. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)|

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya… aikaṃ (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā  khandhā; uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (5)

36. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai  khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

37. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārai ārammaṇasadisā)|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārasadisā)|

38. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā| cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca| (4)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca| (5)

39. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā; vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca…pai॰…| (3)

40. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā; uddhac‍casahagataṃ  aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayādi

41. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – aikaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

42. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… tīṇi… (dassanaina sadisā) anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā|

sahajātapac‍cayau

43. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| dassanaina sadisā )|

44. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā  – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ  pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ  pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca| (5)

45. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā; cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca  vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā mauhau ca| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayādi

46. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

47. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā mauhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, mauhau vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā  dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhā ca mauhau ca vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā… tīṇi (dassanaina sadisā)|

48. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, mauhaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vatthu khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau  uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ mauhaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā…pai॰… (dassanaina sadisaṃ)| (5)

49. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca vippayuttapac‍cayā| vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca  pac‍cayā tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca…pai॰… tīṇi (dassanaina sadisā)|

atthipac‍cayādi
