50. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

51. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai sattarasa, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai sattarasa, samanantarai sattarasa, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai sattārasa nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai sattarasa, puraijātai  sattarasa, āsaivanai sattarasa, kammai sattarasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai sattarasa, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā sattarasa, vigatai sattarasa, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

52. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai (paripuṇṇaṃ) cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

53. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau  uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

54. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ; khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

55. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

56. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā  (sahajātasadisaṃ)… naanantarapac‍cayā … nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā…  napuraijātapac‍cayā (paṭic‍cavārai pac‍canīyasadisaṃ, tairasa pañhā| nin‍nānaṃ)… napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā|

nakammapac‍cayau

57. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā; vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā caitanā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā; uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ pac‍cayā sampayuttakā caitanā| (3)

58. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai  ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā; vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā sampayuttakā caitanā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā; uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca pac‍cayā sampayuttakā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

59. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ, paṭisandhi natthi), naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā rūpajīvitindriyaṃ… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ aikaṃ…pai॰… nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā… (paṭic‍cavārapac‍canīyai navippayuttasadisaṃ, nin‍nānaṃ| aikādasa)| naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

60. nahaituyā  pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai tairasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai aikādasa, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

61. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai  tairasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai aikādasa, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca ( aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

62. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai pañ‍ca, puraijātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai pañ‍ca, indriyai pañ‍ca, jhānai pañ‍ca, maggai pañ‍ca, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā pañ‍ca, natthiyā  pañ‍ca, vigatai pañ‍ca, avigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

63. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

64. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

65. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā mauhau ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau… tīṇi|

66. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ mauhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā sampayuttakā khandhā| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā sampayuttakā khandhā| (3)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayādi

67. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau… aikaṃ|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau  dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā; anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā|

sahajātapac‍cayādi

68. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā … jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

69. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai aikādasa, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai aikādasa, samanantarai aikādasa, sahajātai aikādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nissayai aikādasa, upanissayai aikādasa, puraijātai aikādasa, āsaivanai aikādasa, kammai aikādasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai aikādasa, indriyai aikādasa, jhānai aikādasa, maggai aikādasa, sampayuttai aikādasa, vippayuttai aikādasa, atthiyā aikādasa, natthiyā aikādasa, vigatai aikādasa, avigatai aikādasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

nahaitupac‍cayau

70. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai  khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau|(1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

71. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau…pai॰… naadhipatipac‍cayā (sahajātasadisaṃ)… napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā, naāsaivanapac‍cayā… nakammapac‍cayā… satta, navipākapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā… namaggapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā…|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

74. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

(sampayuttavārau saṃsaṭṭhavārasadisau)|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

75. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau… tīṇi|

76. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

77. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vicikicchaṃ ārabbha vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaina…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā  caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya mauhassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

78. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ  daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti, bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaina…pai॰… bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya mauhassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (5)

79. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā…(vitthāraitabbaṃ dassanattikasadisaṃ) āvaj‍janāya mauhassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā… (yathā dassanattikaṃ)| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā… (yathā dassanattikaṃ)| (3)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhuṃ ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukai khandhai ārabbha uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (5)

80. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vicikicchāsahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

81. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (4)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca mauhañ‍ca ārabbha uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| (5)

adhipatipac‍cayau

82. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (dassanattikasadisaṃ, dasa pañhā)|

anantarapac‍cayau

83. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa  anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

84. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

85. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicikicchāsahagatau  uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau pacchimassa pacchimassa vicikicchāsahagatassa uddhac‍casahagatassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; purimā purimā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa…pai॰… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicikicchāsahagatau  mauhau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau pacchimānaṃ  pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimau purimau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; āvaj‍janā uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

86. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca pacchimassa pacchimassa mauhassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau; vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (dassanaina sadisaṃ gamanaṃ)|

samanantarapac‍cayādi

87. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (anantarasadisaṃ) sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (saṃkhittaṃ| paṭic‍cavārai  sahajātasadisaṃ) añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (saṃkhittaṃ| paṭic‍cavārai añ‍ñamañ‍ñasadisaṃ) nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (saṃkhittaṃ| pac‍cayavārai nissayavārasadisaṃ| visuṃ ghaṭanā natthi)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

88. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, 51anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau… dausau… mauhau…  diṭṭhi… patthanā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā… saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa phalasamāpattiyā mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… diṭṭhi… patthanā vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

89. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… patthanā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa  rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sakabhaṇḍai chandarāgau parabhaṇḍai  chandarāgassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sakapariggahai chandarāgau parapariggahai chandarāgassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa phalasamāpattiyā mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa  ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau…pai॰… patthanā uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

90. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ… mauhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saddhā…pai॰… mauhau saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ  upanissāya diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ… mauhaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mauhau ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti…pai॰… mauhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti| saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mauhau ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā… kāyikaṃ  sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mauhau ca vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mauhau ca uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ  khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

91. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa  rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa phalasamāpattiyā mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

92. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – uddhac‍casahagatā  khandhā ca mauhau ca dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau  ca saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau  ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

puraijātapac‍cayau

93. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ  – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ ārabbha uddhac‍casahagatā  khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu  uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

94. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau  ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

95. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā pacchimassa pacchimassa mauhassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ  pacchimānaṃ vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa (saṃkhittaṃ) tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau…pai॰…| (āsaivanamūlakai vuṭṭhānassapi āvaj‍janāyapi pahātabbaṃ, sattarasa pañhā paripuṇṇā, anantarasadisā)|

kammapac‍cayau

96. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā mauhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

97. bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā mauhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

98. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

99. naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (pavattipaṭisandhi) vipākā khandhā vatthussa…pai॰…|

āhārapac‍cayau

100. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā āhārā  mauhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau… tīṇi (dassanaina sadisaṃ)|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

indriyapac‍cayādi

101. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (āhārasadisaṃ| mauhau kātabbau)|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau… tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…  maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (imai sahaitukā kātabbā) sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍cavārai sampayuttavārasadisaṃ)|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

102. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (dassanattikasadisaṃ)|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (dassanattikasadisaṃ)|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (dassanattikasadisaṃ)| pacchājātā – naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa  ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

103. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰…| pacchājātā – uddhac‍casahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayādi

104. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātā – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā mauhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vicikicchāsahagatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau… tīṇi|

105. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ…pai॰… vatthu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā  khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, vatthu dassanaina pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… vatthu bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vicikicchāsahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ ārabbha vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti…pai॰… vatthuṃ ārabbha…pai॰… vatthu vicikicchāsahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ ārabbha uddhac‍casahagatā  khandhā ca mauhau ca uppaj‍janti…pai॰… vatthuṃ ārabbha…pai॰… vatthu uddhac‍casahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

106. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  …pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatau aikau khandhau ca mauhau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātā – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātā – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca vatthu ca mauhassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vicikicchāsahagatā khandhā ca mauhau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vicikicchāsahagatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ mauhassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatau aikau khandhau ca mauhau ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca mauhau ca…pai॰…| (3)

bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ| tissau pañhā, dassanaina nayaina vibhajitabbā, ‘‘uddhac‍ca’’nti niyāmaitabbaṃ) natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

107. haituyā  aikādasa, ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai sattarasa, samanantarai sattarasa, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai aikavīsa, puraijātai pañ‍ca, pacchājātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai sattarasa, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai aikādasa, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā sattarasa, vigatai sattarasa, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

108. dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

109. bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

110. naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

111. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

(idha sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, missagataṃ atthi, pāḷiyaṃ kātabbaṃ| gaṇanāya upadhāraitvā gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

(idhāpi ārammaṇapac‍cayā upanissayapac‍cayā atthi, pāḷiyaṃ natthi| gaṇaintaina upadhāraitvā gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

(idhāpi  ‘‘sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ’’ yaṃ missakapañhā atthi, pāḷiyaṃ kātabbaṃ)|

112. bhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (idhāpi ‘‘sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ’’ yaṃ missakapañhā atthi )| (2)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (idhāpi ārammaṇaupanissayā atthi)| (3)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau ca dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

(idhāpi sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ atthi| yai tai pañhā na likhitā, tai pāḷiyaṃ gaṇaintānaṃ byañ‍janaina na samainti| tai pāḷiyaṃ na likhitā gaṇanā pākaṭā haunti| yadi saṃsayau uppaj‍jati, anulaumai atthipac‍cayai paikkhitabbaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

113. nahaituyā  aikavīsa, naārammaṇai naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai nasahajātai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nanissayai naupanissayai napuraijātai  napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai nasampayuttai navippayuttai nauatthiyā naunatthiyā nauvigatai nauavigatai sabbattha aikavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

114. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikādasa, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai aikādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai aikādasa, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai pañ‍ca, naunatthiyā aikādasa, nauvigatai aikādasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

115. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai aikavīsa, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai sattarasa, samanantarai sattarasa, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai aikavīsa, puraijātai pañ‍ca, pacchājātai pañ‍ca, āsaivanai sattarasa, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai aikādasa, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā sattarasa, vigatai sattarasa, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pañhāvārau|

dassanainapahātabbahaitukattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. ācayagāmittikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

2. apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – apacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

apacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – apacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

3. naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca  naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

4. ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – apacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī  dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

5. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau… aikaṃ (paṭisandhi natthi); aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca  naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

6. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca, ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā … sahajātapac‍cayā (sabbaipi mahābhūtā kātabbā)… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā (cittasamuṭṭhānampi kaṭattārūpampi upādārūpampi natthi)… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā…  vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

7. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

8. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

9. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau  uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

10. ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī adhipati| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ  khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naanantarapac‍cayādi

11. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā (kusalattikasadisā satta pañhā)… napacchājātapac‍cayā|

naāsaivanapac‍cayau

12. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

apacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā (aikā pañhā sabbai mahābhūtā kātabbā)|

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau  uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

nakammapac‍cayau

13. ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī caitanā| (1)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – apacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī caitanā| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī khandhai paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

14. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ, paṭisandhi natthi)… naāhārapac‍cayā… naindriyapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā… namaggapac‍cayā… nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā (tīṇi)… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

15. nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā cha, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

16. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā cha, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

17. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai dvai, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai dvai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai upanissayai puraijātai āsaivanai kammai dvai, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai dvai, indriyai dvai, jhānai dvai, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai atthiyā natthiyā vigatai avigatai dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

(sahajātavārau paṭic‍cavārasadisau|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

18. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau… tīṇi|

19. naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī khandhā| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī khandhā| (2)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī khandhā| (3)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

20. ācayagāmiñ‍ca  naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca  pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ  khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ācayagāmī khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau… tīṇi|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

21. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau… aikaṃ|

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī khandhā| (1)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī khandhā| (2)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī khandhā| (3)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca  naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – ācayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – apacayagāmiṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

22. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… tīṇi|

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau… tīṇi|

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau… aikaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī khandhā|

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau…pai॰… (idhāpi ghaṭanā haitusadisā)|

anantarapac‍cayādi

23. ācayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apacayagāmī dhammau… tīṇi|
