61. naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā caitanā vipākānaṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

62. saikkhau  dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau saikkhau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… (saikkhamūlakai tīṇi )|

asaikkhau  dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaikkhau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… (asaikkhamūlakai tīṇi)|

āhārapac‍cayādi

63. saikkhau dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

64. saikkhau  dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – saikkhā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saikkhā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (1)|

asaikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saikkhasadisaṃ)|

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ  vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa, vatthu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu saikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu asaikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

atthipac‍cayau

65. saikkhau dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saikkhau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

saikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – saikkhā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saikkhā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saikkhau  dhammau saikkhassa ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa ca dhammassa  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saikkhau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

asaikkhau dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… tīṇi (saikkhasadisaṃ)|

66. naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau  dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ  kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu saikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu asaikkhānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

67. saikkhau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā saikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – saikkhau  aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

saikkhau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – saikkhā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saikkhā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau  āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saikkhā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaikkhau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā asaikkhassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (dvai pañhā kātabbā, saikkhasadisā)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

68. haituyā  satta, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai aṭṭha, samanantarai aṭṭha, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai aṭṭha, vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā aṭṭha, vigatai aṭṭha, avigatai tairasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍canīyuddhārau

69. saikkhau dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saikkhau dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

saikkhau dhammau saikkhassa ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

70. asaikkhau  dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaikkhau dhammau asaikkhassa ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

71. naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau saikkhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau asaikkhassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

72. saikkhau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā saikkhassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

saikkhau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

asaikkhau ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā asaikkhassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

asaikkhau  ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

73. nahaituyā  cuddasa, naārammaṇai naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai cuddasa, nasahajātai dasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nanissayai dasa, naupanissayai tairasa, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai cuddasa, naāsaivanai nakammai cuddasa, navipākai dvādasa, naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai cuddasa, nasampayuttai dasa, navippayuttai aṭṭha, nauatthiyā aṭṭha, naunatthiyā cuddasa, nauvigatai cuddasa, nauavigatai aṭṭha (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

74. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai satta, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

75. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai aṭṭha, samanantarai aṭṭha, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai aṭṭha, vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā  aṭṭha, vigatai aṭṭha, avigatai tairasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pañhāvārau|

saikkhattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. parittattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā| (2)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

2. mahaggataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

3. appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

4. parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai  mahaggataṃ aikaṃ  khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

5. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

6. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – parittaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

7. parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau  uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

8. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā (sabbaipi mahābhūtā kātabbā)… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā … puraijātapac‍cayā (tissau  pañhā kātabbā)… āsaivanapac‍cayā (tissau pañhā kātabbā)… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā (tairasa pañhā)… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā,… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

9. haituyā tairasa, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tairasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai pañ‍ca, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tairasa, vipākai tairasa, āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai tairasa, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, vippayuttai tairasa, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā pañ‍ca, vigatai pañ‍ca, avigatai tairasa|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

10. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

11. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – parittai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai  ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

12. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā| (2)

parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

13. mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca mahaggatādhipati, vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

14. appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca appamāṇādhipati| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

naanantarapac‍cayādi

15. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

16. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… parittai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… (sabbai mahābhūtā vitthāraitabbā, arūpai parittamūlakai tissau pañhā)|

mahaggataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

17. appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|(2)

parittañ‍ca  appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (2)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā  – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

18. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|(1)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā| (2)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

19. mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1) mahaggataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

20. appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

21. parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai  mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau  khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

nakammapac‍cayau

22. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – parittai khandhai paṭic‍ca parittā caitanā| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca mahaggatā caitanā| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca appamāṇā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

23. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti navipākapac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalai appamāṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti navipākapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

parittañ‍ca  appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalai appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naāhārapac‍cayādi

24. parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ (vitthāraitabbaṃ)… naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ, najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ …pai॰… (sabbai mahābhūtā kātabbā), namaggapac‍cayā  – ahaitukaṃ parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… (sabbai mahābhūtā kātabbā) nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…|

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

25. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā dasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai tairasa, naāsaivanai tairasa, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai najhānai namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

26. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā dasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai  dvādasa, napacchājātai tairasa, naāsaivanai  tairasa, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

28. parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… upādārūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā parittā khandhā| (1)

parittaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatā khandhā| (2)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇā khandhā| (3)

parittaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇā  khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatā khandhā| (5)

29. mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇai… tīṇi|

30. parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

parittañ‍ca  appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (2)

parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… appamāṇai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (paṭisandhikkhaṇai tīṇipi kātabbā)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

31. parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ  pac‍cayā khandhā, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā parittā khandhā (avasaisā cha pañhā haitupac‍cayasadisā satta kātabbā), adhipatipac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi, sattarasa pañhā paripuṇṇā), anantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

32. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai satta, kammai sattarasa, vipākai sattarasa, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai jhānai maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

33. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā parittā khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

34. parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ| pañ‍ca)|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

35. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… 53asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā parittā khandhā| (1)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatādhipati, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā mahaggatā khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatā khandhā| (2)

parittaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇādhipati| (3)

parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā mahaggatā  khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (4)

36. mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai pac‍cayā mahaggatādhipati, vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai mahaggatai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai pac‍cayā appamāṇādhipati| (1)

37. parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā appamāṇādhipati| (1)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

parittañ‍ca  mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai  ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā mahaggatādhipati, vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vipākai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vipākai mahaggatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

naanantarapac‍cayādi

38. parittaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā… (paṭic‍cavārasadisā dvādasa pañhā) napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā… (paripuṇṇaṃ, vipākauti niddisitabbaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ vipākauti na kātabbaṃ) nakammapac‍cayā… navipākapac‍cayā… (paṭisandhivipākaupi natthi) naāhārapac‍cayā… naindriyapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā… namaggapac‍cayā… nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

39. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā dvādasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā  pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

40. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā dvādasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

41. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ, anantarai samanantarai sahajātai…pai॰… vigatai avigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pac‍cayavārau|

haitupac‍cayau

42. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…  paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

43. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi)… anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi)… āsaivanapac‍cayā(vipākaupi paṭisandhipi natthi)… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

44. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

45. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

46. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā  – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā mahaggatā adhipati, vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā appamāṇā adhipati| (1)

napuraijātapac‍cayau

47. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

napacchājāta-naāsaivanapac‍cayā

48. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

nakammapac‍cayau

49. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – parittai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā parittā caitanā| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – mahaggatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā mahaggatā caitanā| (1)

appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai appamāṇai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā appamāṇā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

50. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

najhānapac‍cayādi

51. parittaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā… namaggapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai parittaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai mahaggataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai appamāṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

52. nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

53. haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai navippayuttai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇatabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

6. sampayuttavārau

(sampayuttavārau  saṃsaṭṭhavārasadisau)|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

55. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (pavattipaṭisandhi kātabbā)|

appamāṇau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

56. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… parittai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassanti assādainti abhinandanti, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati  …pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau  dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina parittacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| parittā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa yathākammūpagañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

57. mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina mahaggatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa yathākammūpagañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| mahaggatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vippassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

58. appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ maggassa, phalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau  dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā caitaupariyañāṇaina appamāṇacittasamaṅgissa  cittaṃ jānanti, appamāṇā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

59. parittau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… parittai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – parittādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – mahaggatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ…pai॰… dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| mahaggatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati… diṭṭhi  uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – mahaggatādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – mahaggatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

60. appamāṇau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ maggassa… phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – appamāṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – appamāṇādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa ca appamāṇassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – appamāṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

61. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā parittā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… āvaj‍janā parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittaṃ cuticittaṃ mahaggatassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| parittā khandhā mahaggatassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

62. mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā mahaggatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – mahaggataṃ cuticittaṃ parittassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggataṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatā khandhā parittassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

63. appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā appamāṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ appamāṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – phalaṃ parittassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – phalaṃ mahaggatassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

(samanantarapac‍cayaṃ anantarapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

sahajātapac‍cayau

64. parittau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa, vatthu khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; aikaṃ  mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

65. mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

66. appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa ca appamāṇassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau  aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (3)

67. parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā mahaggatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (2)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

68. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

parittau  dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (2)

mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai  mahaggatā khandhā vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (3)

appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā mahaggatassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

69. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu appamāṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

70. mahaggatau  dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

71. appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa ca appamāṇassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (3)

parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau  ca appamāṇau ca dhammā appamāṇassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (2)

parittau  ca mahaggatau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau  ca mahaggatau ca dhammā mahaggatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (2)

upanissayapac‍cayau

72. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parittaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti, parittaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| parittā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ parittāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalākusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇātipātau pāṇātipātassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ)| mātughātikammaṃ mātughātikammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ kusalattikasadisaṃ)| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parittaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya mahaggataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| parittaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya mahaggataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| parittā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mahaggatāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

parittau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau parittaṃ  saddhaṃ upanissāya appamāṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti| parittaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya appamāṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti| parittā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ appamāṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, maggassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa  maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa maggassa…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

73. mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mahaggataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya mahaggataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mahaggataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya mahaggataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mahaggatā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā mahaggatāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mahaggataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti, mahaggataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… mahaggatā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā parittāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya…pai॰… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mahaggataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya appamāṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mahaggataṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya appamāṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mahaggatā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā appamāṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya maggassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

74. appamāṇau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – appamāṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya appamāṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya appamāṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā appamāṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa… maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – appamāṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ… vipassanaṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ… vipassanaṃ… appamāṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā parittāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya…pai॰… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phalasamāpatti kāyikassa sukhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ariyā maggaṃ upanissāya  saṅkhārai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassanti, maggau ariyānaṃ atthappaṭisambhidāya…pai॰… paṭibhānappaṭisambhidāya… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – appamāṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya mahaggataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, appamāṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya  mahaggataṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā mahaggatāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, ariyā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ uppādainti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍janti| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayau

75. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰…  vipassati assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu appamāṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

76. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā parittā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā mahaggatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā appamāṇā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

77. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā parittā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa, anulaumaṃ vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau  dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ tassaiva paṭhamassa jhānassa (?) āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ tassaiva naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa (?) añ‍ñaisu tikaisu aulaukaitabbaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā mahaggatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

78. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – parittā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – parittā caitanā vipākānaṃ parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau  dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – mahaggatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – mahaggatā caitanā vipākānaṃ mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – mahaggatā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā caitanā kaṭattārūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – mahaggatā caitanā kaṭattārūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau  dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – mahaggatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – mahaggatā caitanā vipākānaṃ mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

79. appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – appamāṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – appamāṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ appamāṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa ca appamāṇassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākapac‍cayau

80. parittau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau parittau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (tissau pañhā, pavattipaṭisandhi kātabbā)| (3)

appamāṇau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (pavattimaiva)|

āhārapac‍cayādi

81. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – parittā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – parittā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu appamāṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

82. mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – mahaggatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… pacchājātā – mahaggatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – appamāṇā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – appamāṇā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayau

83. parittau dhammau parittassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – parittau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa atthipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu parittānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – parittā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ  – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, vatthu mahaggatānaṃ khandhānaṃ  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu appamāṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

84. mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – mahaggatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – mahaggatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

85. appamāṇau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – appamāṇā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – appamāṇā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa ca appamāṇassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇau  aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (3)

86. parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – appamāṇā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – appamāṇā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – appamāṇā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā appamāṇassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – appamāṇau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (2)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – mahaggatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – mahaggatā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – mahaggatā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā mahaggatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – mahaggatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai  mahaggatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca…pai॰… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

87. haituyā  satta, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai aikādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai satta, vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai tairasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

88. parittau  dhammau parittassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittau dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

mahaggatau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

mahaggatau dhammau parittassa ca mahaggatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

89. appamāṇau  dhammau appamāṇassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇau dhammau mahaggatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

appamāṇau dhammau parittassa ca appamāṇassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (1)

parittau ca appamāṇau ca dhammā appamāṇassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (2)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā parittassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (1)

parittau ca mahaggatau ca dhammā mahaggatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

90. nahaituyā  pan‍narasa, naārammaṇai pan‍narasa, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai pan‍narasa, nasahajātai dvādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvādasa, nanissayai dvādasa, naupanissayai cuddasa, napuraijātai  cuddasa, napacchājātai pan‍narasa, naāsaivanai pan‍narasa…pai॰… namaggai pan‍narasa, nasampayuttai dvādasa, navippayuttai dasa, nauatthiyā dasa, naunatthiyā pan‍narasa, nauvigatai pan‍narasa, nauavigatai dasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

91. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta…pai॰… namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

13. parittārammaṇattikaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. parittārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai mahaggatārammaṇaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

2. saṅkhyāvārau
