nahaitupac‍cayau

4. parittārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai  khandhā| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā, vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

5. parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau  uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – appamāṇārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

napuraijātapac‍cayādi

6. parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai mahaggatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā (mahaggatārammaṇai paṭisandhi natthi)| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai appamāṇārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā (napacchājātapac‍cayañ‍ca naāsaivanapac‍cayañ‍ca naadhipatisadisaṃ)|

nakammapac‍cayau

7. parittārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – parittārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇā caitanā| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – mahaggatārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇā caitanā| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau  uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – appamāṇārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

8. parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi)… najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā…namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ parittārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…|

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

navippayuttapac‍cayau

mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai mahaggatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai appamāṇārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisau)|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

13. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai parittārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – appamāṇārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

14. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā parittārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| parittārammaṇai parittai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha parittārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina parittārammaṇaparittacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| parittārammaṇā parittā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, parittārammaṇaṃ  iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| parittārammaṇai mahaggatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha mahaggatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina parittārammaṇamahaggatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| parittārammaṇā mahaggatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

15. mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, mahaggatārammaṇaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| mahaggatārammaṇai mahaggatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha mahaggatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina mahaggatārammaṇamahaggatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| mahaggatārammaṇā mahaggatā khandhā  caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭhamajjhānapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbacakkhupac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbasautadhātupac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, iddhividhañāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā mahaggatārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| mahaggatārammaṇai parittai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha parittārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| caitaupariyañāṇaina mahaggatārammaṇaparittacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| mahaggatārammaṇā parittā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

16. appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| caitaupariyañāṇaina appamāṇārammaṇaappamāṇacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| appamāṇārammaṇā appamāṇā khandhā  caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, maggapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| appamāṇārammaṇai parittai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina appamāṇārammaṇaparittacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| appamāṇārammaṇā parittā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā appamāṇārammaṇaṃ caitaupariyañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| caitaupariyañāṇaina appamāṇārammaṇamahaggatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| appamāṇārammaṇaṃ mahaggatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

17. parittārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, parittārammaṇai parittai  khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ  katvā parittārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – parittārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… parittārammaṇaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| parittārammaṇai mahaggatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā mahaggatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

18. mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ…pai॰… mahaggatārammaṇaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ  …pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| mahaggatārammaṇai mahaggatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā mahaggatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – mahaggatārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – paṭhamajjhānapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| mahaggatārammaṇai  parittai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā parittārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

19. appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – appamāṇārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, maggapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – saikkhā appamāṇārammaṇaṃ caitaupariyañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

20. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā parittārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ parittārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – parittārammaṇaṃ cuticittaṃ mahaggatārammaṇassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| parittārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ mahaggatārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| parittārammaṇā khandhā mahaggatārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ appamāṇārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| parittārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

21. mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā mahaggatārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ mahaggatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ cuticittaṃ parittārammaṇassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| mahaggatārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ parittārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatārammaṇā khandhā parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ appamāṇārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahaggatārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ  vaudānassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

22. appamāṇārammaṇau  dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā appamāṇārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ appamāṇārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… phalapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… nibbānapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… appamāṇārammaṇaṃ caitaupariyañāṇaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ parittārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ mahaggatārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… phalapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ mahaggatārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… nibbānapac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ mahaggatārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ mahaggatārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayau

23. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayādi

24. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (paṭic‍cavārasadisā kātabbā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

25. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parittārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… parittārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| parittārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ…pai॰… upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… parittārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| parittārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… parittārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parittārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya mahaggatārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| parittārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ upanissāya mahaggatārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| parittārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ, mahaggatārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

parittārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parittārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya appamāṇārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| parittārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ, rāgaṃ…pai॰… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ upanissāya  appamāṇārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| parittārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ appamāṇārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

26. mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya mahaggatārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| mahaggatārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ, rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya mahaggatārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| mahaggatārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā mahaggatārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, parittārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| mahaggatārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| mahaggatārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā parittārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya appamāṇārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mahaggatārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya appamāṇārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mahaggatārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā appamāṇārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (3)

27. appamāṇārammaṇau  dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – appamāṇārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya appamāṇārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya appamāṇārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti| maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti appamāṇārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā appamāṇārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya maggassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – appamāṇārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, parittārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā parittārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – appamāṇārammaṇaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya mahaggatārammaṇaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇārammaṇaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya mahaggatārammaṇaṃ  jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| appamāṇārammaṇā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā mahaggatārammaṇāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

28. parittārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā parittārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ parittārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau  dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parittārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

29. mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā mahaggatārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ mahaggatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – mahaggatārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

30. appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā appamāṇārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ appamāṇārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

31. parittārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – parittārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – parittārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ parittārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – mahaggatārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – mahaggatārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ mahaggatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā  – mahaggatārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ parittārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

32. appamāṇārammaṇau  dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – appamāṇārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – appamāṇārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ appamāṇārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – appamāṇārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ parittārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākapac‍cayādi

33. parittārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍caya… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍caya… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

34. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai pañ‍ca, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

35. parittārammaṇau  dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

parittārammaṇau  dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

37. appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau appamāṇārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau parittārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau mahaggatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

38. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai nava, nasamanantarai nava, nasahajātai nava, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nanissayai nava, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava…pai॰… namaggai nava, nasampayuttai nava, navippayuttai nava, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā nava, nauvigatai nava, nauavigatai nava (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

39. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai tīṇi…pai॰… namaggai navippayuttai naunatthiyā nauvigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

haitudukaṃ

14. hīnattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. hīnaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca hīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – hīnaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

hīnaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – hīnai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

hīnaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca hīnau ca majjhimau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – hīnaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

2. majjhimaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – majjhimaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

3. paṇītaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca paṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

4. majjhimañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṇītai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

5. hīnañ‍ca majjhimañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – hīnai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

(hīnattikaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhattikasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ|)

hīnattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

15. micchattaniyatattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. micchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

2. sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

3. aniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā  ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtā,| mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

4. micchattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

sammattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sammattaniyatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

5. micchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – micchattaniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – sammattaniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā (sabbai pac‍cayā iminā kāraṇaina vitthāraitabbā| saṃkhittaṃ)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

6. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

7. aniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

8. micchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

9. micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai paṭic‍ca micchattaniyatādhipati| (1)

sammattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – sammattaniyatai khandhai paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatādhipati| (1)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naanantarapac‍cayau

10. micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ, sabbāni pac‍cayāni vitthāraitabbāni)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

11. nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai cha, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

12. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai nasamanantarai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai  naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai  cha, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

13. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikaṃ…pai॰… vigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

14. micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sammattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

15. aniyataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā aniyatā khandhā| (1)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatā khandhā| (2)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatā khandhā| (3)

aniyataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

16. micchattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

micchattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

micchattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – micchattaniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… micchattaniyatai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

sammattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi pañhā, micchattasadisaṃ)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

17. micchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ, kusalattikai pac‍cayavārasadisaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ)… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

18. haituyā  sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai satta, kammai sattarasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

naadhipatipac‍cayau

21. micchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai pac‍cayā micchattaniyatādhipati| (1)

sammattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – sammattaniyatai khandhai pac‍cayā sammattaniyatādhipati| (1)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai  …pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā aniyatā khandhā| (1)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatādhipati| (2)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatādhipati| (3)

micchattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – micchattaniyatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā micchattaniyatādhipati| (1)

sammattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – sammattaniyatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā sammattaniyatādhipati| (1)

aniyataṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai aniyataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

haitupac‍cayau

36. micchattaniyatau  dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – micchattaniyatā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – aniyatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

37. micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā micchattaniyatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| micchattaniyatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassanti| caitaupariyañāṇaina micchattaniyatacittasamaṅgissa  cittaṃ jānanti| micchattaniyatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| caitaupariyañāṇaina sammattaniyatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| sammattaniyatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

38. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ariyā aniyatai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti| pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… aniyatai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti, taṃ ārabbha aniyatau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina  aniyatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rūpajīvitindriyaṃ mātughātikammassa… pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa…  ruhiruppādakammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| yaṃ vatthuṃ parāmasantassa micchattaniyatā khandhā uppaj‍janti, taṃ vatthu micchattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ maggassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

39. micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – micchattaniyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – micchattaniyatādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – micchattaniyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

40. sammattaniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – sammattaniyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā  maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – sammattaniyatādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sammattaniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – sammattaniyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

41. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati  sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā…pai॰… ariyā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ…pai॰… aniyatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā aniyatau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – aniyatādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ maggassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anantarapac‍cayau

42. micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggau phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā aniyatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ aniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

43. aniyatau  dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – aniyataṃ daumanassaṃ micchattaniyatassa daumanassassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatamicchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayādi

44. micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ, nava pañhā)… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ, tissau pañhā)… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (kusalattikasadisā, tairasa  pañhā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

45. micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – mātughātikammaṃ mātughātikammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mātughātikammaṃ…pai॰… pitughātikammaṃ…pai॰… arahantaghātikammaṃ…pai॰… ruhiruppādakammaṃ…pai॰… saṅghabhaidakammaṃ…pai॰… niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ)| niyatamicchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatamicchādiṭṭhi mātughātikammassa…pai॰… saṅghabhaidakammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā…pai॰… arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā…pai॰… duṭṭhaina cittaina tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaitvā…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhinditvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| (2)

46. sammattaniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ariyā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ uppādainti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍janti, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti| maggau ariyānaṃ atthappaṭisambhidāya…pai॰… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

47. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – aniyataṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| aniyataṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| aniyatā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… saināsanaṃ aniyatāya saddhāya…pai॰… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ tassaiva…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ tassaiva…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… pāṇātipātau pāṇātipātassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ)| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – aniyataṃ  rāgaṃ upanissāya mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| aniyataṃ dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| aniyatau rāgau…pai॰… saināsanaṃ mātughātikammassa… pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa… saṅghabhaidakammassa… niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamassa maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa maggassa…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayau

48. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha aniyatau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu aniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – rūpajīvitindriyaṃ mātughātikammassa… pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu micchattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu sammattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

49. micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā micchattaniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā sammattaniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau  dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā aniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

50. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā aniyatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ aniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ  vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – aniyataṃ daumanassaṃ micchattaniyatassa daumanassassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aniyatamicchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kammapac‍cayau

51. micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – micchattaniyatā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – micchattaniyatā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

micchattaniyatau  dhammau micchattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

52. sammattaniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – sammattaniyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – sammattaniyatā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – sammattaniyatā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sammattaniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sammattaniyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – aniyatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – aniyatā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

53. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aniyatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰…|

āhārapac‍cayādi

54. micchattaniyatau  dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

55. micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – micchattaniyatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – micchattaniyatā  khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – sammattaniyatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – sammattaniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – aniyatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu aniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – aniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa  vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu micchattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu sammattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

atthipac‍cayau

56. micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – micchattaniyatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – micchattaniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – micchattaniyatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (tissau pañhā)|

aniyatau  dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – aniyatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya  sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu aniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – aniyatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – rūpajīvitindriyaṃ mātughātikammassa…pai॰… ruhiruppādakammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu micchattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu sammattaniyatānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

57. micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā micchattaniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – micchattaniyatau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca…pai॰…| (1)

micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā aniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – micchattaniyatā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – micchattaniyatā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca  imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – micchattaniyatā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sammattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā sammattaniyatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (dvai pañhā micchattaniyatasadisā)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

58. haituyā  satta, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā aṭṭha, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai  tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā pañ‍ca, vigatai pañ‍ca, avigatai tairasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍canīyuddhārau

59. micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

micchattaniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

micchattaniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sammattaniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sammattaniyatau  dhammau sammattaniyatassa ca aniyatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

60. aniyatau dhammau aniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

aniyatau dhammau micchattaniyatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

aniyatau dhammau sammattaniyatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā micchattaniyatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

micchattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā aniyatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

sammattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā sammattaniyatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

sammattaniyatau ca aniyatau ca dhammā aniyatassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

61. nahaituyā  tairasa, naārammaṇai naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai tairasa, nasahajātai nava, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nanissayai nava, naupanissayai tairasa, napuraijātai aikādasa, napacchājātai tairasa, naāsaivanai tairasa, nakammai navipākai naāhārai tairasa…pai॰…  namaggai tairasa, nasampayuttai nava, navippayuttai satta, nauatthiyā satta, naunatthiyā tairasa, nauvigatai tairasa, nauavigatai satta (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

62. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta…pai॰… namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ
