16. maggārammaṇattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. maggārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

2. maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – maggahaitukaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

3. maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (4)

maggādhipatiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (5)

4. maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggārammaṇañ‍ca  maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

5. maggahaitukañ‍ca  maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

6. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā… anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

7. haituyā  sattarasa, ārammaṇai adhipatiyā anantarai samanantarai sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai upanissayai puraijātai āsaivanai kammai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai vippayuttai atthiyā natthiyā vigatai avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

8. maggārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

9. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā  – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

10. maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukādhipati| (1)

maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggādhipati adhipati| (2)

maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca adhipati| (3)

11. maggādhipatiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggādhipatī khandhai paṭic‍ca maggādhipati adhipati| maggādhipatiṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggādhipatī khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau adhipati| (3)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (4)

maggādhipatiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggādhipatī khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca adhipati| (5)

12. maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau  khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

13. maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggahaitukai ca maggādhipatī ca khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau adhipati| (1)

maggahaitukañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggahaitukai ca maggādhipatī ca khandhai paṭic‍ca maggādhipati adhipati| (2)

maggahaitukañ‍ca  maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – maggahaitukai ca maggādhipatī ca khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca adhipati| (3)

napuraijātapac‍cayādi

14. maggārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇā dvaipi)|

naāsaivanapac‍cayau

15. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

16. maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā  – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggādhipatiṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

17. maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā  – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

maggārammaṇañ‍ca  maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipatī tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (2)

maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naāsaivanapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (3)

nakammapac‍cayau

18. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā caitanā| (1)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggādhipati  caitanā| (2)

maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nakammapac‍cayā – maggārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipati ca caitanā| (3)

19. maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – maggahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukā caitanā| (1)

maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – maggahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggādhipati caitanā| (2)

maggahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nakammapac‍cayā – maggahaitukai khandhai paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca caitanā| (3)

20. maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – maggādhipatī khandhai paṭic‍ca maggādhipati caitanā (pañ‍ca pañhā)|

maggārammaṇañ‍ca maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā (paṭhamaghaṭanai tīṇi)|

maggahaitukañ‍ca  maggādhipatiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā (dutiyaghaṭanai tīṇi pañhā)|

navipākapac‍cayau

21. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ)|

namaggapac‍cayau

22. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ maggārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau  khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

navippayuttapac‍cayau

23. maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ, arūpanti niyāmaitabbaṃ)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

24. nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, napuraijātai sattarasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai sattarasa, navipākai sattarasa, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

25. haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā sattarasa, napuraijātai sattarasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai sattarasa, navipākai navippayuttai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

26. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ…pai॰… jhānai sampayuttai vippayuttai atthiyā natthiyā vigatai avigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍cayānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(sahajātavāraupi  pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

27. maggārammaṇau  dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – maggārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (iminā kāraṇaina sattarasa pañhā kātabbā)|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

28. maggahaitukau dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina maggahaitukacittasamaṅgissa  cittaṃ jānanti, maggahaitukā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggahaitukau  dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (2)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ  katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (3)

29. maggādhipati dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (1)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina maggādhipaticittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti, maggādhipatī khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (3)

30. maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina maggahaitukamaggādhipaticittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti, maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggādhipatissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ  garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (2)

maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

31. maggārammaṇau  dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggārammaṇānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

32. maggahaitukau dhammau maggahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (2)

maggahaitukau  dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – maggahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (4)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggahaitukassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggahaitukādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggahaitukānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

33. maggādhipati  dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – maggādhipati adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati  – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – maggādhipati adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggādhipati dhammau maggahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggādhipati adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – maggādhipati adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggārammaṇānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

maggādhipati dhammau maggahaitukassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggādhipati adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggāhaitukānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

34. maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggārammaṇau  ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggādhipatissa dhammassa  adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggārammaṇānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

35. maggahaitukau  ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (1)

maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggahaitukassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggahaitukānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggādhipatissa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggahaitukau  ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (4)

maggahaitukau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggahaitukassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – maggahaitukā ca maggādhipatī ca adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ maggahaitukānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

anantarapac‍cayau

36. maggārammaṇau  dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āvaj‍janā maggārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āvaj‍janā maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggārammaṇānañ‍ca  maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āvaj‍janā maggārammaṇānañ‍ca  maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

37. maggādhipati dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggādhipatī khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggādhipatī khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggādhipatī khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggārammaṇānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

38. maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – purimā purimā maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggārammaṇau ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggādhipatissa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggādhipatīnaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggārammaṇau  ca maggādhipati ca dhammā maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā maggārammaṇā ca maggādhipatī ca khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ maggārammaṇānañ‍ca maggādhipatīnañ‍ca khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayādi

39. maggārammaṇau dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (anantarasadisaṃ) sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (tīsupi sattarasa pañhā kātabbā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

40. maggārammaṇau  dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cavaikkhaṇā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggārammaṇau dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cavaikkhaṇā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇūpanissayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (2)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇūpanissayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| (4)

maggahaitukau dhammau maggahaitukassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

42. maggādhipati  dhammau maggādhipatissa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; pac‍cavaikkhaṇā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cavaikkhaṇā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

maggādhipati dhammau maggahaitukassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

maggādhipati dhammau maggārammaṇassa ca maggādhipatissa ca dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cavaikkhaṇā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

54. nahaituyā aikavīsa, naārammaṇai sattarasa (naārammaṇai gahitai pakatārammaṇampi upanissayārammaṇampi dvaipi chij‍janti), naadhipatiyā  aikavīsa, naanantarai nasamanantarai nasahajātai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nanissayai naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai nasampayuttai navippayuttai nauatthiyā naunatthiyā nauvigatai nauavigatai aikavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

55. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai sattarasa, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai naāhārai naindriyai najhānai namaggai navippayuttai naunatthiyā nauvigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

56. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikavīsa, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai sattarasa, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai aikavīsa, āsaivanai nava, kammai sattarasa, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā nava, vigatai nava, avigatai sattarasa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

maggārammaṇattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

17. uppan‍nattikaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai uppan‍nā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

2. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍naṃ cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| uppan‍naṃ sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… uppan‍nai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… uppan‍nā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

3. anuppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anuppan‍nai rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… anuppan‍nai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ  uppaj‍jati| anuppan‍nā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

4. uppādī dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppādiṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… uppādī khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| uppādī khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

5. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – uppan‍naṃ cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| uppan‍naṃ sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… uppan‍nai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰…| sahajātādhipati – uppan‍nā adhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anuppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – anuppan‍nai rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… anuppan‍nai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (1)

uppādī  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – uppādiṃ  cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… uppādī khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (1)

sahajātapac‍cayau

6. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtā kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

7. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai uppan‍nau  aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtā…pai॰…| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

8. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu uppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

upanissayapac‍cayau

9. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰… pakatūpanissayau – uppan‍naṃ utuṃ upanissāya jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| uppan‍naṃ bhaujanaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya jhānaṃ uppādaiti vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| uppan‍naṃ utu…  bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ uppan‍nāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anuppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – anuppan‍naṃ  vaṇṇasampadaṃ patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| anuppan‍naṃ saddasampadaṃ… gandhasampadaṃ… rasasampadaṃ… phauṭṭhabbasampadaṃ… anuppan‍nai khandhai patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| anuppan‍nā vaṇṇasampadā…pai॰… anuppan‍nā khandhā uppan‍nāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

uppādī dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – uppādiṃ cakkhusampadaṃ patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| uppādiṃ sautasampadaṃ…pai॰… kāyasampadaṃ…pai॰… vaṇṇasampadaṃ … gandhasampadaṃ… rasasampadaṃ… phauṭṭhabbasampadaṃ… uppādī khandhai patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| uppādī cakkhusampadā…pai॰… kāyasampadā… vaṇṇasampadā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbasampadā… uppādī khandhā uppan‍nāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

puraijātapac‍cayau

10. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu uppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

11. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā uppan‍nā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

12. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai uppan‍nā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

13. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai  uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āhārapac‍cayau

14. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

indriyapac‍cayau

15. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – uppan‍nā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

jhānapac‍cayādi

16. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – uppan‍nā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai uppan‍nā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa, vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu uppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – uppan‍nā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayau

17. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – uppan‍nau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautudhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu uppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – uppan‍nā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

avigatapac‍cayau

18. uppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

19. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai aikaṃ, pacchājātai kammai vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai vippayuttai atthiyā avigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ  gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

20. uppan‍nau  dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anuppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

uppādī dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

21. nahaituyā tīṇi, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi, nauatthiyā dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi, nauavigatai dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

22. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… naunatthiyā nauvigatai aikaṃ|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

23. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai aikaṃ, pacchājātai aikaṃ, kammai vipākai āhārai indriyai jhānai  maggai sampayuttai vippayuttai atthiyā avigatai aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

uppan‍nattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

18. atītattikaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. pac‍cuppan‍nau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pac‍cuppan‍nā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

2. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| atītaṃ cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| atītaṃ sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… atītai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau  vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā… uddhac‍caṃ… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| atītā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

3. anāgatau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgataṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… anāgatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| anāgatā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pac‍cuppan‍naṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… pac‍cuppan‍nai  khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

4. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā…pai॰… pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| atītaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… atītai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (1) anāgatau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – anāgataṃ  cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… anāgatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – pac‍cuppan‍naṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… pac‍cuppan‍nai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – pac‍cuppan‍nādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anantarapac‍cayau

5. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā atītā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ  vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

samanantarapac‍cayau

6. atītau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)| (1)

sahajātapac‍cayādi

7. pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

upanissayapac‍cayau

8. atītau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – atītaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ… maggaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| atītaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| atītā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… pac‍cuppan‍nāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – anāgataṃ cakkhusampadaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… sautasampadaṃ… ghānasampadaṃ jivhāsampadaṃ… kāyasampadaṃ… vaṇṇasampadaṃ… saddasampadaṃ… gandhasampadaṃ… rasasampadaṃ… phauṭṭhabbasampadaṃ…patthayamānau…pai॰… anāgatai khandhai patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ… anāgatā cakkhusampadā…pai॰… vaṇṇasampadā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbasampadā… anāgatā khandhā pac‍cuppan‍nāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cuppan‍naṃ  utuṃ upanissāya jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍naṃ bhaujanaṃ saināsanaṃ upanissāya jhānaṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| pac‍cuppan‍naṃ utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya kāyikassa…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

puraijātapac‍cayau

9. pac‍cuppan‍nau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

10. pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

11. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā atītā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

12. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – atītā caitanā pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pac‍cuppan‍nā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ  khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pac‍cuppan‍nā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

13. pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – vipākau pac‍cuppanau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āhārapac‍cayādi

14. pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… vatthu pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayau

15. pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (uppan‍nattikai atthisadisaṃ)| (1)

natthivigatāvigatapac‍cayā

16. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa avigatapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

17. haituyā  aikaṃ, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai pacchājātai āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai dvai, vipākai āhārai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

18. atītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

19. nahaituyā tīṇi, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi  …pai॰… nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, nauatthiyā dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi, nauavigatai dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

20. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai naupanissayai…pai॰… nasampayuttai navippayuttai naunatthiyā nauvigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

21. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai samanantarai sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai aikaṃ, pacchājātai āsaivanai aikaṃ…pai॰… kammai dvai, vipākai aikaṃ (imaisu padaisu aikaṃyaiva), avigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pañhāvārau|

atītattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

19. atītārammaṇattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. atītārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

2. anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anāgatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

3. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

4. atītārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā (adhipatiyā paṭisandhi natthi)… anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā (puraijātaipi āsaivanaipi paṭisandhi natthi)… kammapac‍cayā…  vipākapac‍cayā (vipākaṃ atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ, tissaupi pañhā paripuṇṇā| pavattipaṭisandhi kātabbā)… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

5. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… (sabbattha tīṇi), vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

6. atītārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai  …pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

7. anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ anāgatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

8. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

9. atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (anulaumasahajātasadisaṃ)|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

10. atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai anāgatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

11. atītārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā (naadhipatisadisā)… nakammapac‍cayā – atītārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇā caitanā| (1)

anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – anāgatārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇā  caitanā| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇai khandhai paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

12. atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā…pai॰… (navipākai paṭisandhi natthi)|

najhānapac‍cayau

13. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

namaggapac‍cayau

14. atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… (nahaitusadisā tissau pañhā, mauhau natthi )|

navippayuttapac‍cayau

15. atītārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai atītārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai anāgatārammaṇaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰…| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

16. nahaituyā  tīṇi, naadhipatiyā napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai  dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

17. haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

18. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… (sabbattha tīṇi) avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

19. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – atītārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai atītārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgatārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā  haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

20. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – atītaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| atītārammaṇaṃ atītaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā atītārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| atītārammaṇai atītai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha atītārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| atītārammaṇā atītā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| atītārammaṇaṃ anāgataṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| atītārammaṇai anāgatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰…  vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha anāgatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| atītārammaṇā anāgatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atītārammaṇau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina atītārammaṇapac‍cuppan‍nacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| atītārammaṇā pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

21. anāgatārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgatārammaṇaṃ anāgataṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgatārammaṇai anāgatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha anāgatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| anāgatārammaṇā anāgatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgatārammaṇaṃ atītaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ…pai॰… ariyā anāgatārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti| pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| anāgatārammaṇai atītai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha atītārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| anāgatārammaṇā atītā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina anāgatārammaṇapac‍cuppan‍nacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| anāgatārammaṇā pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

22. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – caitaupariyañāṇaina pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇapac‍cuppan‍nacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā pac‍cuppan‍nā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau  dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – atītaṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ atītaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇai atītai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha atītārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā atītā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgataṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ anāgataṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇai anāgatai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati…pai॰… taṃ ārabbha anāgatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā anāgatā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

23. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – atītaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| atītārammaṇaṃ atītaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| atītārammaṇai atītai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā atītārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – atītārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  …pai॰… atītārammaṇaṃ anāgataṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… atītārammaṇai anāgatai khandhai garuṃ  katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā anāgatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

24. anāgatārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – anāgatārammaṇaṃ anāgataṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| anāgatārammaṇai anāgatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā anāgatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – anāgatārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – anāgatārammaṇaṃ atītaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰…  anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… anāgatārammaṇai atītai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā atītārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

25. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – atītaṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ atītaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇai atītai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā atītārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – anāgataṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ  sautadhātuṃ garuṃ katvā  …pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ anāgataṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇai anāgatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā anāgatārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

26. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – purimā purimā atītārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ atītārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – atītārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ anāgatārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atītārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – atītārammaṇaṃ cuticittaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa paṭisandhicittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| atītārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

27. anāgatārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā anāgatārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ anāgatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anāgatārammaṇaṃ iddhividhañāṇaṃ atītārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ atītārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ atītārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa…pai॰… anāgatārammaṇā khandhā atītārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

28. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa  anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ paṭisandhicittaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa bhavaṅgassa…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa bhavaṅgassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau  dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ paṭisandhicittaṃ atītārammaṇassa bhavaṅgassa…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ atītārammaṇassa bhavaṅgassa…pai॰… pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā khandhā atītārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayau

29. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayādi

30. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…(tayaupi pac‍cayā paṭic‍cavārasadisā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

31. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – atītārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā atītārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – atītārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā anāgatārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atītārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – atītārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

32. anāgatārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – anāgatārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā anāgatārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – anāgatārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā atītārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – anāgatārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

33. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā atītārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā anāgatārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

34. atītārammaṇau  dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā atītārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ atītārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā anāgatārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ anāgatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

35. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – atītārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – atītārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ atītārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – atītārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ anāgatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atītārammaṇau  dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – atītārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

36. anāgatārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – anāgatārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – anāgatārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ anāgatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau  dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – anāgatārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ atītārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – anāgatārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

37. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā  – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… nānākkhaṇikā – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ atītārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā  – pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ anāgatārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākapac‍cayādi

38. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

39. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai  nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tīṇi  (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

40. atītārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atītārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

atītārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

41. anāgatārammaṇau dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anāgatārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

42. pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇassa dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau atītārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau  dhammau anāgatārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

43. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai nava, nasamanantarai nava (saṃkhittaṃ, sabbattha nava), nauvigatai nava, nauavigatai nava (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

44. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā naanantarai nasamanantarai naupanissayai napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai nakammai navipākai tīṇi (sabbattha tīṇi, saṃkhittaṃ), naunatthiyā nauvigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

45. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai indriyai jhānai maggai sampayuttai atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tīṇi (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pañhāvārau|

atītārammaṇattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

20. ajjhattattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. ajjhattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

2. ajjhattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

3. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

bahiddhā  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

4. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā, samanantarapac‍cayā, sahajātapac‍cayā – ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca  tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayādi

5. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā (puraijātaipi āsaivanaipi paṭisandhi natthi)… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā…  sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ
