nahaitupac‍cayau

7. ajjhattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ ajjhattaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

8. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā  – ajjhattai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai ajjhattai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – bahiddhā khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai bahiddhā khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

9. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… (anulaumasahajātasadisaṃ, nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ) naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā … naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā … naupanissayapac‍cayā, napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… ajjhattai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai ajjhattaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…|

10. bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai bahiddhā aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… bahiddhā khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai (paripuṇṇaṃ) aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…|

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

11. ajjhattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā, naāsaivanapac‍cayā, nakammapac‍cayā – ajjhattai khandhai paṭic‍ca ajjhattā caitanā| āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – bahiddhā khandhai paṭic‍ca bahiddhā caitanā| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…| (1)

navipākapac‍cayādi

12. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā (paṭisandhi natthi), naāhārapac‍cayā – utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ| (1)

najhānapac‍cayau

13. ajjhattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ…pai॰… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ…pai॰…  bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

namaggapac‍cayādi

14. ajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā… (nahaitusadisaṃ| mauhau natthi) nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai…pai॰… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… naunatthipac‍cayā, nauvigatapac‍cayā|

bahiddhā dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ), cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā bahiddhā khandhā| (1)

bahiddhā dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā (pavattipaṭisandhipi mahābhūtāpi kātabbā) – cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā bahiddhā khandhā| vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

pac‍cayavārau

4-6. nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(nissayavārau pac‍cayavārasadisau| saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi vitthāraitabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

27. ajjhattau  dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

bahiddhā  dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bahiddhā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

28. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā  maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… ajjhattai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautudhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau  dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parau ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… ajjhattai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| caitaupariyañāṇaina ajjhattacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ bahiddhā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

29. bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – parau dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai  suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti… nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai…pai॰… parau bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… bahiddhā khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| caitaupariyañāṇaina bahiddhā cittasamaṅgissa…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhā rūpāyatanaṃ bahiddhā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… bahiddhā khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ  uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| caitaupariyañāṇaina bahiddhā cittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| bahiddhā rūpāyatanaṃ  ajjhattaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ajjhattaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

30. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā, taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… ajjhattai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ  garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – ajjhattādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – parau ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… ajjhattai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

31. bahiddhā  dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – parau dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā, taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… phalaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… bahiddhā khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati  – bahiddhādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ… bahiddhā khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti, abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau…pai॰… diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayau

32. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā ajjhattā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa, anulaumaṃ vaudānassa, gautrabhu maggassa, vaudānaṃ maggassa, maggau phalassa, phalaṃ phalassa, anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā, niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā  dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (purimā purimā bahiddhāti nānākaraṇaṃ, taṃyaiva gamanaṃ)| (1)

samanantarapac‍cayādi

33. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… (anantarasadisaṃ)… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

upanissayapac‍cayau

34. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ajjhattaṃ saddhaṃ  upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… jhānaṃ…pai॰… vipassanaṃ…pai॰… maggaṃ…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| ajjhattaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| ajjhattā saddhā…pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… saināsanaṃ ajjhattāya saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parau ajjhattaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| parau ajjhattaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| ajjhattā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ bahiddhā saddhāya…pai॰… maggassa phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

35. bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – parau bahiddhā saddhaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ  upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati bahiddhā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ bahiddhā saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bahiddhā saddhaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| bahiddhā saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ ajjhattāya saddhāya…pai॰… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayau

36. ajjhattau  dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…  kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – parau ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ bahiddhā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

37. bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – parau bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| bahiddhā rūpāyatanaṃ bahiddhā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – bahiddhā cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ…pai॰… vatthu bahiddhā khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā  dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| bahiddhā rūpāyatanaṃ ajjhattassa cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ajjhattassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (2)

38. ajjhattau  ca bahiddhā ca dhammā ajjhattassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| bahiddhā rūpāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ cakkhāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattassa cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ kāyāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā rūpāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ vatthu ca…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ vatthu ca ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau ca bahiddhā ca dhammā bahiddhā dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā cakkhāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā kāyāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā vatthu ca…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā vatthu ca bahiddhā khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

39. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā ajjhattā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – pacchājātā bahiddhā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

40. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā ajjhattā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā  dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā…pai॰… (ajjhattasadisaṃyaiva)|

kammapac‍cayau

41. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – ajjhattā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – ajjhattā caitanā vipākānaṃ ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – bahiddhā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – bahiddhā caitanā vipākānaṃ bahiddhā khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

42. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰… (paripuṇṇaṃ, paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)|

āhārapac‍cayau

43. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau ajjhattassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau bahiddhā kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bahiddhā  dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (pavattipaṭisandhi) bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau bahiddhā kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ajjhattassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

44. ajjhattau dhammau ca bahiddhā dhammau ca ajjhattassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca ajjhattassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau dhammau ca bahiddhā dhammau ca bahiddhā dhammassa  āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca bahiddhā kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

indriyapac‍cayādi

45. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, ajjhattikā indriyā (rūpajīvitindriyampi vitthāraitabbaṃ)… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (mātikāpadāni anumaj‍jantaina vitthāraitabbāni)|

bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

atthipac‍cayau

46. ajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – ajjhattau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa  …pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ…pai॰…| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ (puraijātasadisaṃ), vatthu ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – ajjhattā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau ajjhattassa kāyassa…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

ajjhattau dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – puraijātaṃ, āhāraṃ| puraijātaṃ – parau ajjhattaṃ cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau bahiddhā kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

47. bahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ (bahiddhā nin‍nānākaraṇaṃ, mātikāpadāni vitthāraitabbāni)| (1)

bahiddhā dhammau ajjhattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ, āhāraṃ| puraijātaṃ – bahiddhā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, bahiddhā rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ajjhattassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ajjhattassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

48. ajjhattau dhammau ca bahiddhā dhammau ca ajjhattassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ, āhāraṃ| puraijātaṃ – bahiddhā rūpāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ cakkhu ca ajjhattassa cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ kāyāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhā rūpāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ vatthu ca…pai॰… bahiddhā phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ vatthu  ca ajjhattānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āhāraṃ – ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca ajjhattassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattau  dhammau ca bahiddhā dhammau ca bahiddhā dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ, āhāraṃ| puraijātaṃ – ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā cakkhāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ  phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā kāyāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattaṃ rūpāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā vatthu ca bahiddhā khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca bahiddhā vatthu ca bahiddhā khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āhāraṃ – ajjhattau kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca bahiddhā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca bahiddhā kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthivigatāvigatapac‍cayā

suddhaṃ

anulaumaṃ|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ
