haitupac‍cayau

11. ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai ajjhattārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau  dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bahiddhārammaṇā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

12. ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, ajjhattārammaṇaṃ ajjhattaṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā ajjhattārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| ajjhattārammaṇai ajjhattai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha ajjhattārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| ajjhattārammaṇā ajjhattā khandhā pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – bahiddhā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ  pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ajjhattārammaṇaṃ bahiddhā dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, ajjhattārammaṇai bahiddhā khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaina ajjhattārammaṇabahiddhācittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, ajjhattārammaṇā bahiddhā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

13. bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bahiddhārammaṇaṃ bahiddhā dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| iddhividhañāṇaṃ…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ…pai॰… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| bahiddhārammaṇai bahiddhā khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati…pai॰… caitaupariyañāṇaina bahiddhārammaṇabahiddhācittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| bahiddhārammaṇā  bahiddhā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ  katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, bahiddhārammaṇai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| bahiddhārammaṇaṃ ajjhattaṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ… iddhividhañāṇaṃ… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ… bahiddhārammaṇai ajjhattai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha ajjhattārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| bahiddhārammaṇā ajjhattā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

14. ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ajjhattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| ajjhattārammaṇaṃ ajjhattaṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ…pai॰… iddhividhañāṇaṃ… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… ajjhattārammaṇai ajjhattai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā ajjhattārammaṇau rāgau  uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – ajjhattārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

15. bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – bahiddhārammaṇādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau  dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ… phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| bahiddhārammaṇaṃ ajjhattaṃ dibbaṃ cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… dibbaṃ sautadhātuṃ… iddhividhañāṇaṃ… caitaupariyañāṇaṃ… pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ… yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ… anāgataṃsañāṇaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, bahiddhārammaṇai ajjhattai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā ajjhattārammaṇau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayau

16. ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā ajjhattārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ ajjhattārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – ajjhattārammaṇaṃ cuticittaṃ bahiddhārammaṇassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ bahiddhārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattārammaṇā khandhā bahiddhārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ajjhattārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

17. bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā bahiddhārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ bahiddhārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – bahiddhārammaṇaṃ cuticittaṃ ajjhattārammaṇassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhārammaṇaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ ajjhattārammaṇāya āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| bahiddhārammaṇā khandhā ajjhattārammaṇassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayādi

18. ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

upanissayapac‍cayau

19. ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ajjhattārammaṇā  anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā, ajjhattārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ajjhattārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā bahiddhārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

20. bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bahiddhārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā bahiddhārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – bahiddhārammaṇā anic‍cānupassanā, dukkhānupassanā, anattānupassanā ajjhattārammaṇāya anic‍cānupassanāya, dukkhānupassanāya, anattānupassanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

21. ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā ajjhattārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ ajjhattārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ajjhattārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa, anulaumaṃ vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā  bahiddhārammaṇā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ bahiddhārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bahiddhārammaṇaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

22. ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – ajjhattārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – ajjhattārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ ajjhattārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – ajjhattārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ bahiddhārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – bahiddhārammaṇā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – bahiddhārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ bahiddhārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – bahiddhārammaṇā caitanā vipākānaṃ ajjhattārammaṇānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākapac‍cayādi

23. ajjhattārammaṇau  dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

24. haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai dvai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai dvai, upanissayai cattāri, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai cattāri, vipākai dvai …pai॰… (sabbattha dvai ), sampayuttai dvai, atthiyā dvai, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

25. ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau bahiddhārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau ajjhattārammaṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

26. nahaituyā  cattāri, naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai cattāri (saṃkhittaṃ, sabbattha cattāri), napuraijātai napacchājātai naāsaivanai…pai॰… navippayuttai cattāri…pai॰… nauavigatai cattāri (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

27. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai dvai, naadhipatiyā dvai, naanantarai nasamanantarai naupanissayai naāsaivanai nakammai…pai॰… naunatthiyā nauvigatai dvai (sabbattha dvai| aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

28. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nissayai dvai, upanissayai cattāri, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai cattāri, vipākai dvai…pai॰… sampayuttai dvai, atthiyā dvai, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai dvai (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pañhāvārau|

ajjhattārammaṇattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

22. sanidassanasappaṭighattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ| (2)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca āpaudhātu, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (3)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, āpaudhātu, indriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau  āhārau| (4)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā āpaudhātu ca, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ āpaudhātu ca| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ, āpaudhātu, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (5)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| (6)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ, āpaudhātu, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (7)

2. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau  khandhā anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca  khandhā| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ| (2)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| (3)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā| sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (4)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca  anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (5)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| (6)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ  paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (7)

3. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca  anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| (2)

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (3)

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (4)

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai  anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭatārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (5)

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai  khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| (6)

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| (7)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

4. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai  …pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca  khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

5. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

(anidassanasappaṭighamūlakai iminā kāraṇaina satta pañhā vibhajitabbā, pariyausānapadā natthi|)

6. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(iminā kāraṇaina anidassanaappaṭighamūlakai satta pañhā vibhajitabbā, niṭṭhānapadā natthi|)

7. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| anidassanaappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(iminā kāraṇaina sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

anantara-samanantarapac‍cayā

8. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau  uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā (ārammaṇasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

9. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā…pai॰…|

(anidassanasappaṭighamūlakā satta pañhā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā|)

10. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(anidassanaappaṭighamūlakai satta pañhā iminā kāraṇaina kātabbā|)

11. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca  anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(iminā kāraṇaina satta pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

12. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca āpaudhātu| (2)

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā āpaudhātu ca, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ āpaudhātu ca| bāhiraṃ…pai॰…| (3)

13. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vatthu ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighā mahābhūtā| bāhiraṃ…pai॰…| (2)

14. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| bāhiraṃ…pai॰…| (1)

nissayapac‍cayādi

15. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

16. haituyā aikavīsa, ārammaṇai aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikavīsa, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikavīsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai aikavīsa, upanissayai aikaṃ, puraijātai aikaṃ, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai aikavīsa, vipākai āhārai aikavīsa, indriyai aikavīsa, jhānai  maggai aikavīsa, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikavīsa, atthiyā aikavīsa, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

17. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

(anidassanasappaṭighamūlakai iminā kāraṇaina sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

18. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca  itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau|

(anidassanaappaṭighamūlakā iminā kāraṇaina satta pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

19. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau  uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(iminā kāraṇaina satta pañhā vitthāraitabbā asammauhantaina|)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

20. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ…, utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(anidassanasappaṭighamūlakā iminā kāraṇaina sattapi pañhā vitthāraitabbā|)

21. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(anidassanaappaṭighamūlakai  iminā kāraṇaina sattapi pañhā vitthāraitabbā|)

22. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai  ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpāyatanaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(ghaṭanai iminā kāraṇaina sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

naadhipatipac‍cayādi

23. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (sahajātasadisaṃ)… naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(iminā kāraṇaina aikavīsa pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā… nakammapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ upādārūpaṃ (kammaṃ vibhajitvā nakammainaiva aikavīsa pañhā  kātabbā), navipākapac‍cayā (paṭisandhipi kaṭattāpi natthi, pañ‍cavaukāraiyaiva kātabbā), naāhārapac‍cayā  – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… (iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā aikavīsāpi)|

naindriyapac‍cayādi

24. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ, sabbai pañhā vibhajitabbā)… najhānapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ, sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā)|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(aivaṃ sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

25. anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…, asañ‍ñasattānaṃ…pai॰… anidassanasappaṭighai mahābhūtai ca āpaudhātuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

(aivaṃ sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā), namaggapac‍cayā… (nahaitusadisaṃ kātabbaṃ| paripuṇṇaṃ| mauhau natthi)| nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā (paripuṇṇaṃ)… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

26. nahaituyā  aikavīsa, naārammaṇai aikavīsa, naadhipatiyā aikavīsa  (saṃkhittaṃ, sabbattha aikavīsa), naunatthiyā aikavīsa, nauvigatai aikavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

27. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikavīsa, naadhipatiyā aikavīsa…pai॰… nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai aikavīsa, nasampayuttai aikavīsa, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā aikavīsa, nauvigatai aikavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

28. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikavīsa  …pai॰… jhānai aikavīsa, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikavīsa, atthiyā aikavīsa, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2-6. sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi paṭic‍cavārasadisā, saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi arūpaiyaiva kātabbā|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

29. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā haitū sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

(anidassanaappaṭighamūlakaiyaiva  iminā kāraṇaina satta pañhā vibhajitabbā|)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

30. sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rūpai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā  rūpaṃ passati, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sanidassanasappaṭighā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… anidassanasappaṭighā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

31. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti…pai॰… vatthuṃ… itthindriyaṃ… purisindriyaṃ… jīvitindriyaṃ… āpaudhātuṃ… kabaḷīkāraṃ āhāraṃ… anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, caitaupariyañāṇaina anidassanaappaṭighacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighā  khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

32. sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – rūpaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (1)

33. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… phalā vuṭṭhahitvā…pai॰… phalaṃ garuṃ katvā…pai॰… nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā… nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthuṃ… itthindriyaṃ… purisindriyaṃ… jīvitindriyaṃ… āpaudhātuṃ… kabaḷīkāraṃ āhāraṃ… anidassanaappaṭighai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – anidassanaappaṭighādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – anidassanaappaṭighādhipati sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(anidassanaappaṭighamūlakai sattapi pañhā vibhajitabbā, adhipati tividharūpasaṅgahaina|)

anantarapac‍cayau

34. anidassanaappaṭighau  dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

samanantarapac‍cayau

35. anidassanaappaṭighau  dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayādi

36. anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ sādhukaṃ kātabbaṃ| añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayai paṭic‍cavārai añ‍ñamañ‍ñasadisaṃ, nissayapac‍cayai paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

37. sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – vaṇṇasampadaṃ patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ…pai॰… vaṇṇasampadā saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – cakkhusampadaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… kāyasampadaṃ… saddasampadaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbasampadaṃ patthayamānau dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ… utuṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti sīlaṃ samādiyati| upausathakammaṃ, jhānaṃ… vipassanaṃ… maggaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| cakkhusampadā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbasampadā, utu, saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

38. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ… jhānaṃ… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ  jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… bhaujanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| saddhā …pai॰… pañ‍ñā, rāgau…pai॰… patthanā, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… bhaujanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñāya, maggassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

puraijātapac‍cayau

39. sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – rūpai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi…pai॰… vicikicchā…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā  rūpaṃ passati, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… saddai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – vatthuṃ… itthindriyaṃ… purisindriyaṃ… jīvitindriyaṃ… āpaudhātuṃ… kabaḷīkāraṃ āhāraṃ… anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

40. sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| rūpāyatanañ‍ca vatthu ca anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau  ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| cakkhāyatanañ‍ca vatthu ca…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca vatthu ca anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| rūpāyatanañ‍ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

41. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā puraijātassa imassa anidassanaappaṭighassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – pacchājātā anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā puraijātassa imassa sanidassanasappaṭighassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(aivaṃ satta pañhā vibhajitabbā, tividharūpasaṅgahau|) (7)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

42. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu  maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

43. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – anidassanaappaṭighā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – anidassanaappaṭighā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – anidassanaappaṭighā caitanā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – anidassanaappaṭighā caitanā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(aivaṃ satta pañhā sahajātā nānākkhaṇikā iminā kāraṇaina vibhajitabbā, tividharūpasaṅgahau|) (7)

vipākapac‍cayau

44. anidassanaappaṭighau  dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau anidassanaappaṭighau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa  vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(aivaṃ satta pañhā vitthāraitabbā, pavattipaṭisandhi|) (7)

āhārapac‍cayau

45. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ  anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa anidassanaappaṭighassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā āhārā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā āhārā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa sanidassanasappaṭighassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(aivaṃ satta pañhā  pavattipaṭisandhi vibhajitabbā, sattasupi kabaḷīkārau āhārau kātabbau|) (7)

indriyapac‍cayau

46. anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā indriyā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā indriyā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(aivaṃ pavattipaṭisandhi satta pañhā vibhajitabbā, rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca antai antai|) (7)

47. anidassanasappaṭighau  ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhundriyañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ  indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… kāyindriyañ‍ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

jhānapac‍cayādi

48. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

49. anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ  khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā puraijātassa imassa anidassanaappaṭighassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – anidassanaappaṭighā  khandhā puraijātassa imassa sanidassanasappaṭighassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(avasaisā pañ‍ca pañhā aivaṃ vitthāraitabbā| sahajātā, pacchājātā|)

atthipac‍cayau

50. sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – rūpai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

51. anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anidassanasappaṭighā mahābhūtā anidassanasappaṭighānaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ cakkhāyatanassa…pai॰… rasāyatanassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| utusamuṭṭhānā mahābhūtā anidassanasappaṭighānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| asañ‍ñasattānaṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā…pai॰…| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

(paṭic‍cavārai nissayapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātā – anidassanasappaṭighā mahābhūtā anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (yāva asañ‍ñasattā vitthāraitabbā)| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ, saddai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

(avasaisā  cattārau pañhā vitthāraitabbā| paṭic‍cavārai sahajātapac‍cayasadisā, nin‍nānākaraṇā|) (7)

52. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – anidassanaappaṭighau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… āpaudhātu anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ…pai॰… āpaudhātu itthindriyassa…pai॰… kabaḷīkārāhārassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ āpaudhātu anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthuṃ… itthindriyaṃ… purisindriyaṃ… jīvitindriyaṃ… āpaudhātuṃ… kabaḷīkāraṃ āhāraṃ… anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, vatthu anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā puraijātassa imassa anidassanaappaṭighassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa anidassanaappaṭighassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (aivaṃ avasaisā cha pañhā vibhajitabbā| sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyampi kātabbā)| (7)

53. sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – rūpāyatanañ‍ca vatthu ca anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca sanidassanasappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai… (saṃkhittaṃ, asañ‍ñasattānañ‍ca kātabbā)| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ)| (2)

54. anidassanasappaṭighau  ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātā – anidassanaappaṭighā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… (yāva asañ‍ñasattā kātabbā)| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanañ‍ca vatthu ca…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca vatthu ca anidassanaappaṭighānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (avasaisā catassau pañhā vibhajitabbā|) (7)

55. sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – rūpāyatanañ‍ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau  ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| rūpāyatanañ‍ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

(natthivigatapac‍cayaṃ anantarasadisaṃ| avigatapac‍cayaṃ atthisadisaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

56. haituyā  satta, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikavīsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai aikavīsa, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai cha, pacchājātai satta, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai satta, vipākai satta, āhārai satta, indriyai nava, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aṭṭha, atthiyā pañ‍cavīsa, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai pañ‍cavīsa|

haitusabhāgaṃ

57. haitupac‍cayā adhipatiyā satta, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai satta, vipākai satta, indriyai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai satta, atthiyā satta, avigatai satta|

haitusāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā (9)

58. haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti  satta| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ñanissaya-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vippayutta-atthi  -avigatanti satta (avipākaṃ-4)|

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti satta| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissayavipāka-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-sampayutta-atthiavigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti satta| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ (savipākaṃ-5)|

(aivaṃ sabbau gaṇanavārau gaṇaitabbau|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍canīyuddhārau

59. sanidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau anidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa  ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

anidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanasappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

anidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

60. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau anidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (5)

anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanasappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

anidassanaappaṭighau  dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

61. sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātaṃ| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (3)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca  dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanasappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā sanidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanasappaṭighassa ca anidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7)

62. sanidassanasappaṭighau  ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa puraijātaṃ| (1)

sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

63. nahaituyā  pañ‍cavīsa, naārammaṇai dvāvīsa, naadhipatiyā pañ‍cavīsa, naanantarai pañ‍cavīsa, nasamanantarai pañ‍cavīsa, nasahajātai dvādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai catuvīsa, nanissayai nava, naupanissayai pañ‍cavīsa, napuraijātai bāvīsa, napacchājātai pañ‍cavīsa, naāsaivanai pañ‍cavīsa, nakammai pañ‍cavīsa, navipākai catuvīsa, naāhārai pañ‍cavīsa, naindriyai taivīsa, najhānai pañ‍cavīsa, namaggai pañ‍cavīsa, nasampayuttai catuvīsa, navippayuttai bāvīsa, nauatthiyā  nava, naunatthiyā pañ‍cavīsa, nauvigatai pañ‍cavīsa, nauavigatai nava|

nahaitudukaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai bāvīsa (paṭhamagamanasadisaṃ), nauavigatai nava|

nahaitutikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā bāvīsa, naanantarai bāvīsa, nasamanantarai bāvīsa, nasahajātai nava, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai bāvīsa, nanissayai nava, naupanissayai aikavīsa, napuraijātai bāvīsa, napacchājātai bāvīsa…pai॰… nasampayuttai bāvīsa, navippayuttai bāvīsa, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā bāvīsa, nauvigatai bāvīsa, nauavigatai nava (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

64. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta…pai॰… nasampayuttai satta, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta|

haitusāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā

65. haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti  naārammaṇai satta…pai॰… naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta (idhāpi saṃkhittaṃ), nasampayuttai satta, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta|

haitu  -sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai aikaṃ (sabbattha aikaṃ), nauvigatai aikaṃ (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

66. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikavīsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai aikavīsa, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai cha, pacchājātai satta, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai satta, vipākai satta, āhārai satta, indriyai nava, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aṭṭha, atthiyā pañ‍cavīsa, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai pañ‍cavīsa (aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|
